                    <div>
                        <label class="block text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1.5 md:mb-2">클릭 시 이동할 링크 URL (선택사항)</label>
                        <input type="url" id="banner-link" placeholder="예) https://talent.unifactory.kr/market.html" class="w-full p-3 md:p-4 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-100 rounded-lg md:rounded-xl focus:border-rosegold-400 outline-none font-medium text-sm md:text-base transition">
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label class="block text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1.5 md:mb-2">노출 순서 (낮을수록 먼저 노출)</label>
                        <input type="number" id="banner-order" value="1" min="1" class="w-full p-3 md:p-4 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-100 rounded-lg md:rounded-xl focus:border-rosegold-400 outline-none font-medium text-sm md:text-base font-bold text-rosegold-600 transition">
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1.5 md:mb-2">노출 상태</label>
                    <select id="banner-status" class="w-full p-3 md:p-4 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-100 rounded-lg md:rounded-xl focus:border-rosegold-400 outline-none font-medium text-gray-700 text-sm md:text-base transition">
                        <option value="active">즉시 노출 (운영 중)</option>
                        <option value="scheduled">노출 대기 (예약)</option>
                        <option value="ended">노출 종료</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <button onclick="submitBanner()" class="w-full bg-rosegold-500 text-white font-black text-lg md:text-xl py-4 md:py-5 mt-2 rounded-xl md:rounded-[1.25rem] hover:bg-rosegold-600 transition shadow-lg active:scale-95">배너 등록/저장하기</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        lucide.createIcons();
    </script>

    <!-- 🔥 파이어베이스 실시간 데이터 연동 (배너 관리) 🔥 -->
    <script type="module">
        import { db, auth, appId } from '../firebase-config.js';
        import { collection, onSnapshot, updateDoc, doc, deleteDoc, addDoc, serverTimestamp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js";
        import { onAuthStateChanged, signInWithEmailAndPassword, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js";

        window.openLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.remove('hidden');
        window.closeLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.add('hidden');

        window.logoutAdmin = () => {
            if(confirm("관리자 계정에서 로그아웃 하시겠습니까?")) {
                signOut(auth).then(() => location.reload());
            }
        };

        window.loginAdmin = () => {
            const id = document.getElementById('login-id').value;
            const pw = document.getElementById('login-pw').value;
            if (id === 'admin') {
                signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, 'admin@internal.system', pw).then(() => { 
                    closeLoginModal(); 
                }).catch(() => alert('비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.'));
            } else { 
                alert('ID 확인 (admin)'); 
            }
        };

        window.toggleAll = (source) => {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.banner-checkbox');
            checkboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = source.checked);
        };

        const getSelectedIds = () => {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.banner-checkbox:checked');
            return Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => cb.value);
        };

        window.activateSelected = async () => {
            const ids = getSelectedIds();
            if (ids.length === 0) return alert("활성화할 배너를 먼저 선택해주세요.");
            if (!confirm(`선택한 ${ids.length}개의 배너를 '노출 중' 상태로 일괄 변경하시겠습니까?`)) return;

            try {
                for (const id of ids) {
                    await updateDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'banners', id), { 
                        status: 'active' 
                    });
                }
                alert("✅ 노출 활성화 처리가 완료되었습니다.");
                document.getElementById('check-all').checked = false;
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert("❌ 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다. 권한을 확인해주세요.");
            }
        };

        window.deactivateSelected = async () => {
            const ids = getSelectedIds();
            if (ids.length === 0) return alert("노출을 중지할 배너를 먼저 선택해주세요.");
            if (!confirm(`선택한 ${ids.length}개의 배너를 '노출 종료' 상태로 일괄 변경하시겠습니까?\n(더 이상 메인 홈페이지에 보이지 않습니다.)`)) return;

            try {
                for (const id of ids) {
                    await updateDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'banners', id), { 
                        status: 'ended' 
                    });
                }
                alert("✅ 노출 중지 처리가 완료되었습니다.");
                document.getElementById('check-all').checked = false;
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert("❌ 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다. 권한을 확인해주세요.");
            }
        };

        window.deleteSelected = async () => {
            const ids = getSelectedIds();
            if (ids.length === 0) return alert("삭제할 배너를 먼저 선택해주세요.");
            if (!confirm(`경고: 선택한 ${ids.length}개의 배너를 데이터베이스에서 완전히 삭제합니다.\n이 작업은 되돌릴 수 없습니다. 진행할까요?`)) return;

            try {
                for (const id of ids) {
                    await deleteDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'banners', id));
                }
                alert("🗑️ 삭제 처리가 완료되었습니다.");
                document.getElementById('check-all').checked = false;
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert("❌ 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다. 권한을 확인해주세요.");
            }
        };

        let selectedBannerMedia = null;
        let selectedMediaType = 'image';
        let editingBannerId = null;

        window.openBannerModal = (bannerId = null) => {
            editingBannerId = bannerId;
            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-banner');
            const titleEl = document.getElementById('banner-modal-title');
            
            if (bannerId) {
                titleEl.innerHTML = `<i data-lucide="edit" class="text-rosegold-500 w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i> 팝업/배너 수정`;
                const banner = window.currentBanners.find(b => b.id === bannerId);
                if(banner) {
                    document.getElementById('banner-title').value = banner.title || '';
                    document.getElementById('banner-link').value = banner.linkUrl || '';
                    document.getElementById('banner-start').value = banner.startDate || '';
                    document.getElementById('banner-end').value = banner.endDate || '';
                    document.getElementById('banner-position').value = banner.position || 'main_popup';
                    document.getElementById('banner-status').value = banner.status || 'active';
                    document.getElementById('banner-order').value = banner.order || 1;
                    
                    selectedBannerMedia = banner.imageUrl || null;
                    selectedMediaType = banner.mediaType || (selectedBannerMedia && selectedBannerMedia.startsWith('data:video') ? 'video' : 'image');
                    
                    const imgPreview = document.getElementById('banner-media-preview-img');
                    const vidPreview = document.getElementById('banner-media-preview-vid');

                    if (selectedBannerMedia) {
                        if (selectedMediaType === 'video') {
                            imgPreview.classList.add('hidden');
                            vidPreview.src = selectedBannerMedia;
                            vidPreview.classList.remove('hidden');
                        } else {
                            vidPreview.classList.add('hidden');
                            imgPreview.src = selectedBannerMedia;
                            imgPreview.classList.remove('hidden');
                        }
                    } else {
                        imgPreview.src = ''; imgPreview.classList.add('hidden');
                        vidPreview.src = ''; vidPreview.classList.add('hidden');
                    }
                }
            } else {
                titleEl.innerHTML = `<i data-lucide="image-plus" class="text-rosegold-500 w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i> 새 팝업/배너 등록`;
                document.getElementById('banner-title').value = '';
                document.getElementById('banner-link').value = '';
                document.getElementById('banner-start').value = '';
                document.getElementById('banner-end').value = '';
                document.getElementById('banner-position').value = 'main_popup';
                document.getElementById('banner-status').value = 'active';
                document.getElementById('banner-order').value = 1;
                
                selectedBannerMedia = null;
                selectedMediaType = 'image';
                
                const imgPreview = document.getElementById('banner-media-preview-img');
                const vidPreview = document.getElementById('banner-media-preview-vid');
                imgPreview.src = ''; imgPreview.classList.add('hidden');
                vidPreview.src = ''; vidPreview.classList.add('hidden');
            }
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            lucide.createIcons();
        };

        window.closeBannerModal = () => {
            document.getElementById('modal-banner').classList.add('hidden');
        };

        document.getElementById('banner-media-input').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            const file = e.target.files[0];
            if (file) {
                selectedMediaType = file.type.startsWith('video/') ? 'video' : 'image';
                const reader = new FileReader();
                
                reader.onload = (event) => {
                    selectedBannerMedia = event.target.result;
                    const imgPreview = document.getElementById('banner-media-preview-img');
                    const vidPreview = document.getElementById('banner-media-preview-vid');

                    if (selectedMediaType === 'video') {
                        imgPreview.classList.add('hidden');
                        vidPreview.src = selectedBannerMedia;
                        vidPreview.classList.remove('hidden');
                    } else {
                        vidPreview.classList.add('hidden');
                        imgPreview.src = selectedBannerMedia;
                        imgPreview.classList.remove('hidden');
                    }
                };
                reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            }
        });

        window.submitBanner = async () => {
            const title = document.getElementById('banner-title').value;
            const position = document.getElementById('banner-position').value;
            const startDate = document.getElementById('banner-start').value;
            const endDate = document.getElementById('banner-end').value;
            const linkUrl = document.getElementById('banner-link').value;
            const status = document.getElementById('banner-status').value;
            const order = parseInt(document.getElementById('banner-order').value) || 1;

            if (!title) return alert("배너 제목을 입력해주세요!");
            if (!selectedBannerMedia && !editingBannerId) return alert("배너 사진이나 영상을 등록해주세요!");

            const bannerData = {
                title, position, startDate, endDate, linkUrl, status, order,
                mediaType: selectedMediaType || 'image',
                updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
            };
            if (selectedBannerMedia) bannerData.imageUrl = selectedBannerMedia;

            try {
                if (editingBannerId) {
                    await updateDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'banners', editingBannerId), bannerData);
                    alert("✅ 성공적으로 수정되었습니다!");
                } else {
                    bannerData.clicks = 0;
                    bannerData.createdAt = serverTimestamp();
                    await addDoc(collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'banners'), bannerData);
                    alert("✅ 성공적으로 새 배너가 등록되었습니다!\n(메인 홈페이지에서 확인해보세요.)");
                }
                closeBannerModal();
            } catch(e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert("❌ 저장 중 오류가 발생했습니다. 권한 설정을 확인하세요.");
            }
        };

        // 실시간 동기화
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('banner-list-body');

            if (user && user.email === 'admin@internal.system') {
                const bannersRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'banners');
                onSnapshot(bannersRef, (snapshot) => {
                    const banners = [];
                    let activeCount = 0;
                    let scheduledCount = 0;
                    let totalClicks = 0;

                    snapshot.forEach(doc => {
                        const data = doc.data();
                        banners.push({ id: doc.id, ...data });
                        if (data.status === 'active') activeCount++;
                        if (data.status === 'scheduled') scheduledCount++;
                        totalClicks += (data.clicks || 0);
                    });
                    
                    banners.sort((a, b) => {
                        const orderA = parseInt(a.order) || 1;
                        const orderB = parseInt(b.order) || 1;
                        
                        if (orderA !== orderB) return orderA - orderB; 
                        
                        const tA = (a.createdAt && typeof a.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? a.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                        const tB = (b.createdAt && typeof b.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? b.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                        return tB - tA; 
                    });
                    
                    window.currentBanners = banners; 

                    document.getElementById('stat-total-banners').innerText = banners.length;
                    document.getElementById('stat-total-bottom').innerText = banners.length;
                    document.getElementById('stat-active-banners').innerText = activeCount;
                    document.getElementById('stat-scheduled-banners').innerText = scheduledCount;
                    document.getElementById('stat-total-clicks').innerText = totalClicks.toLocaleString();

                    if (banners.length === 0) {
                        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="9" class="px-6 py-12 text-center text-gray-500 font-medium">현재 등록된 배너가 없습니다.<br><span class="text-sm mt-1 inline-block text-gray-400">상단의 '새 팝업/배너 등록' 버튼을 눌러 추가해주세요.</span></td></tr>`;
                    } else {
                        tbody.innerHTML = banners.map(banner => {
                            let statusBadge = '';
                            if (banner.status === 'active') statusBadge = '<span class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">노출 중</span>';
                            else if (banner.status === 'scheduled') statusBadge = '<span class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">대기 중</span>';
                            else statusBadge = '<span class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">종료됨</span>';

                            let thumbHtml = '';
                            if (banner.imageUrl) {
                                if (banner.mediaType === 'video' || banner.imageUrl.startsWith('data:video')) {
                                    thumbHtml = `<video src="${banner.imageUrl}" class="w-full h-full object-cover" muted loop autoplay playsinline></video>`;
                                } else {
                                    thumbHtml = `<img src="${banner.imageUrl}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">`;
                                }
                            } else {
                                thumbHtml = `<i data-lucide="image" class="w-5 h-5"></i>`;
                            }

                            return `
                                <tr class="border-b border-gray-50 hover:bg-rosegold-50/30 transition ${banner.status === 'ended' ? 'opacity-60 bg-gray-50' : ''}">
                                    <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center"><input type="checkbox" value="${banner.id}" class="banner-checkbox rounded border-gray-300 text-rosegold-500 focus:ring-rosegold-500 cursor-pointer"></td>
                                    <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center font-black text-rosegold-500 text-sm md:text-base">
                                        ${banner.order || 1}
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4">
                                        <div class="w-16 h-10 rounded-lg bg-gray-100 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400 border border-gray-200">
                                            ${thumbHtml}
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4">
                                        <p class="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm md:text-base truncate max-w-[150px] md:max-w-[200px] mb-0.5">${banner.title || '제목 없음'}</p>
                                        <a href="${banner.linkUrl || '#'}" target="_blank" class="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-blue-500 hover:underline truncate inline-block max-w-[150px] md:max-w-[200px]">링크: ${banner.linkUrl || '설정안됨'}</a>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-gray-600 text-xs md:text-sm whitespace-nowrap">${banner.position === 'main_popup' ? '메인 팝업 (중앙)' : (banner.position || '미지정')}</td>
                                    <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-gray-500 text-[10px] md:text-xs whitespace-nowrap">${banner.startDate || '-'} ~ ${banner.endDate || '-'}</td>
                                    <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center font-bold text-gray-600 text-xs md:text-sm">${(banner.clicks || 0).toLocaleString()}</td>
                                    <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center whitespace-nowrap">${statusBadge}</td>
                                    <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center">
                                        <button onclick="openBannerModal('${banner.id}')" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-rosegold-600 transition" title="상세보기/수정"><i data-lucide="edit" class="w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5 mx-auto"></i></button>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            `;
                        }).join('');
                        lucide.createIcons();
                    }
                }, (error) => console.error("배너 데이터 가져오기 실패:", error));
            } else {
                if(tbody) tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="9" class="px-6 py-20 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium italic">관리자 로그인이 필요합니다. 상단의 로그인 버튼을 눌러주세요.</td></tr>';
                openLoginModal(); 
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ko">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>부산이음 - 동아리 관리</title>
    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/lucide@latest"></script>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Noto+Sans+KR:wght@300;400;500;700;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
    <!-- 🌟 공통 메뉴/헤더/로그인 컴포넌트 불러오기 -->
    <script src="components.js"></script>

    <script>
        tailwind.config = {
            theme: {
                extend: {
                    fontFamily: { sans: ['"Noto Sans KR"', 'sans-serif'] },
                    colors: {
                        rosegold: {
                            50: '#fff0f0', 100: '#ffe4e6', 200: '#fecdd3', 300: '#fda4af',
                            400: '#fb7185', 500: '#e58e8e', 600: '#d97777', 700: '#be123c', 800: '#6c4a4a'
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    </script>
    <style>
        ::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 8px; height: 8px; }
        ::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: #f1f1f1; }
        ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: #e58e8e; border-radius: 10px; }
        ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover { background: #d97777; }
        .hide-scrollbar::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }
        .hide-scrollbar { -ms-overflow-style: none; scrollbar-width: none; }
    </style>
</head>
<body class="bg-gray-50 text-gray-800 font-sans antialiased h-screen flex overflow-hidden">

    <!-- 🌟 1. 공통 사이드바 컴포넌트 -->
    <admin-sidebar active="clubs"></admin-sidebar>

    <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col h-screen overflow-hidden w-full">
        
        <!-- 🌟 2. 공통 헤더 컴포넌트 -->
        <admin-header title="동아리 관리"></admin-header>

        <main class="flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-8 bg-gray-50">
            
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:gap-6 mb-6 md:mb-8">
                <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-4 hover:-translate-y-1 transition-transform">
                    <div class="w-12 h-12 md:w-14 md:h-14 rounded-2xl bg-rosegold-50 flex items-center justify-center text-rosegold-500 flex-shrink-0">
                        <i data-lucide="camera" class="w-6 h-6 md:w-7 md:h-7"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm text-gray-500 font-bold mb-0.5">전체 개설 동아리</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-total-clubs" class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-gray-800">0</h3>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6 shadow-sm border border-yellow-100 flex items-center gap-4 hover:-translate-y-1 transition-transform relative overflow-hidden">
                    <div class="absolute -right-4 -top-4 w-20 h-20 bg-yellow-50 rounded-full z-0"></div>
                    <div class="w-12 h-12 md:w-14 md:h-14 rounded-2xl bg-yellow-100 flex items-center justify-center text-yellow-600 relative z-10 flex-shrink-0">
                        <i data-lucide="alert-circle" class="w-6 h-6 md:w-7 md:h-7"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div class="relative z-10">
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm text-yellow-600 font-bold mb-0.5">신규 개설 승인 대기</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-pending-clubs" class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-yellow-600">0</h3>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
                <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                    <table class="w-full text-left border-collapse whitespace-nowrap">
                        <thead>
                            <tr class="bg-gray-50 text-gray-500 text-sm border-b border-gray-100">
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium w-20">이미지</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">동아리명 (분류)</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">개설자 (리더)</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-center">상태</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-center">관리</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody id="club-list-body" class="text-sm text-gray-700">
                            <tr>
                                <td colspan="5" class="px-6 py-20 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium">
                                    <div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
                                        <div class="animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-b-2 border-rosegold-500"></div>
                                        데이터를 불러오는 중입니다.
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </main>
    </div>

    <!-- 🌟 3. 공통 로그인 모달 컴포넌트 -->
    <admin-login-modal></admin-login-modal>

    <script type="module">
        import { db, auth, appId } from '../firebase-config.js';
        import { collection, onSnapshot, doc, updateDoc, deleteDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js";
        import { onAuthStateChanged, signInWithEmailAndPassword, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js";

        lucide.createIcons();

        // UI 제어 함수
        window.openLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.remove('hidden');
        window.closeLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.add('hidden');

        window.logoutAdmin = () => {
            if(confirm("관리자 계정에서 로그아웃 하시겠습니까?")) {
                signOut(auth).then(() => location.reload());
            }
        };

        window.loginAdmin = () => {
            const id = document.getElementById('login-id').value;
            const pw = document.getElementById('login-pw').value;
            if (id === 'admin') {
                signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, 'admin@internal.system', pw).then(() => { 
                    closeLoginModal(); 
                }).catch(() => alert('비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.'));
            } else { 
                alert('ID 확인 (admin)'); 
            }
        };

        onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
            const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login');
            const prof = document.getElementById('user-profile');
            const tbody = document.getElementById('club-list-body');

            if (user && user.email === 'admin@internal.system') {
                if(btn) btn.classList.add('hidden'); 
                if(prof) { prof.classList.remove('hidden'); prof.classList.add('flex'); }
                startListening();
            } else {
                if(btn) btn.classList.remove('hidden'); 
                if(prof) prof.classList.add('hidden');
                
                if(tbody) tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="px-6 py-20 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium italic">관리자 로그인이 필요합니다. 상단의 로그인 버튼을 눌러주세요.</td></tr>';
                openLoginModal(); 
            }
        });

        const startListening = () => {
            // 사이드바 배지 실시간 동기화
            onSnapshot(collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market'), (snap) => {
                let count = 0; snap.forEach(d => { if(d.data().status === 'pending') count++; });
                const b = document.getElementById('badge-pending-posts');
                if(b) { if(count > 0) { b.innerText = count+'건'; b.classList.remove('hidden'); } else b.classList.add('hidden'); }
            });
            onSnapshot(collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'reports'), (snap) => {
                let count = 0; snap.forEach(d => { if(d.data().status === 'pending') count++; });
                const b = document.getElementById('badge-pending-reports');
                if(b) { if(count > 0) { b.innerText = count+'건'; b.classList.remove('hidden'); } else b.classList.add('hidden'); }
            });

            // 🌟 동아리 리스트 동기화
            onSnapshot(collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs'), snap => {
                const list = []; 
                let pending = 0;
                
                snap.forEach(d => { 
                    const data = d.data(); 
                    list.push({id: d.id, ...data}); 
                    if(data.status === 'pending') pending++; 
                });
                
                const statTotal = document.getElementById('stat-total-clubs');
                const statPending = document.getElementById('stat-pending-clubs');
                if(statTotal) statTotal.innerText = snap.size;
                if(statPending) statPending.innerText = pending;

                const tbody = document.getElementById('club-list-body');
                if(!tbody) return;

                if (list.length === 0) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="py-20 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium">개설된 동아리가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
                } else {
                    list.sort((a,b) => {
                        if (a.status === 'pending' && b.status !== 'pending') return -1;
                        if (a.status !== 'pending' && b.status === 'pending') return 1;
                        const tA = (a.createdAt && typeof a.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? a.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                        const tB = (b.createdAt && typeof b.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? b.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                        return tB - tA;
                    });

                    tbody.innerHTML = list.map(c => `
                        <tr class="border-b border-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-50 transition">
                            <td class="px-6 py-4">
                                <div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-gray-100 border border-gray-200 shadow-sm flex-shrink-0">
                                    ${c.imageUrl ? `<img src="${c.imageUrl}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">` : `<div class="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400"><i data-lucide="image" class="w-5 h-5"></i></div>`}
                                </div>
                            </td>
                            <td class="px-6 py-4">
                                <p class="font-bold text-gray-800 text-base">${c.name}</p>
                                <p class="text-[10px] font-bold text-rosegold-400 mt-1 inline-block px-2 py-0.5 bg-rosegold-50 rounded-md border border-rosegold-100">${c.category === 'son' ? '손수 (수공예)' : (c.category === 'mom' ? '몸소 (활동)' : '그 외 (스터디)')}</p>
                            </td>
                            <td class="px-6 py-4 text-gray-600 font-medium">
                                <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                                    <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-blue-50 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-black text-blue-500 shadow-inner flex-shrink-0">${(c.leaderName||'이').charAt(0)}</div>
                                    ${c.leaderName || '이웃'}
                                </div>
                            </td>
                            <td class="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                <span class="px-3 py-1.5 ${c.status === 'active' ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-200' : 'bg-yellow-50 text-yellow-600 border-yellow-200'} border rounded-full text-xs font-black tracking-wide shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap">
                                    ${c.status === 'active' ? '운영 중' : '승인 대기'}
                                </span>
                            </td>
                            <td class="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                <div class="flex justify-center gap-2">
                                    ${c.status === 'pending' ? `<button onclick="updateClub('${c.id}', 'active')" class="px-3 md:px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-sm hover:bg-emerald-600 active:scale-95 transition whitespace-nowrap">가입 승인</button>` : ''}
                                    <button onclick="deleteClub('${c.id}')" class="px-3 md:px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 text-red-500 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-sm hover:bg-red-50 active:scale-95 transition whitespace-nowrap">해체(삭제)</button>
                                </div>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    `).join('');
                    lucide.createIcons();
                }
            });
        };

        // 관리자 권한 기능: 동아리 승인 및 삭제
        window.updateClub = async (id, status) => {
            if(!confirm('해당 동아리 개설을 승인하시겠습니까?')) return;
            try { 
                await updateDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs', id), { status }); 
                alert('✅ 동아리 개설이 승인되었습니다!'); 
            } catch(e) { alert('승인에 실패했습니다. 관리자 권한을 확인해주세요.'); } 
        };
        
        window.deleteClub = async (id) => { 
            if(confirm('정말 이 동아리를 삭제(해체)할까요? 관련된 게시글도 모두 접근 불가 처리됩니다.')) {
                try { 
                    await deleteDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs', id)); 
                    alert('🗑️ 동아리가 삭제되었습니다.'); 
                } catch(e) { alert('삭제에 실패했습니다.'); }
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

// 1. 공통 사이드바(메뉴) 컴포넌트
class AdminSidebar extends HTMLElement {
    connectedCallback() {
        // 현재 활성화된 메뉴 항목을 받아옵니다. (기본값: index)
        const active = this.getAttribute('active') || 'index';
        
        // 선택된 메뉴만 색상을 반전시키는 헬퍼 함수
        const getMenuClass = (menuId) => {

            const inactiveClass = "flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4 px-3 md:px-4 py-2.5 md:py-3 rounded-lg md:rounded-xl text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 hover:text-rosegold-600 font-bold text-sm md:text-base transition relative";
            return active === menuId ? activeClass : inactiveClass;
        };

        this.innerHTML = `
            <aside id="admin-sidebar" class="fixed md:static inset-y-0 left-0 transform -translate-x-full md:translate-x-0 w-72 bg-white border-r border-gray-200 flex flex-col transition-transform duration-300 shadow-[4px_0_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.02)] z-40">

                    </button>
                </div>

                <nav class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto py-4 md:py-6 hide-scrollbar">
                    <ul class="space-y-1 md:space-y-2 px-3 md:px-4">
                        <li>
                            <a href="index.html" class="${getMenuClass('index')}">
                                <i data-lucide="layout-dashboard" class="w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5"></i> 전체현황

                            <a href="members.html" class="${getMenuClass('members')}">
                                <i data-lucide="users" class="w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5"></i> 이웃(회원) 관리
                            </a>
                        </li>
                        <li>
                            <a href="clubs.html" class="${getMenuClass('clubs')}">
                                <i data-lucide="camera" class="w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5"></i> 동아리 관리
                            </a>
                        </li>
                        <li>
                            <a href="market.html" class="${getMenuClass('market')}">
                                <i data-lucide="store" class="w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5"></i> 동네 소식 관리
                                <span id="badge-pending-posts" class="hidden ml-auto bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full shadow-sm">0건</span>
                            </a>
                        </li>
                        <li class="pt-3 md:pt-4 pb-1 px-3 md:px-4">
                            <span class="text-[10px] md:text-xs font-extrabold text-gray-400 tracking-wider">기타 업무</span>
                        </li>
                        <li>
                            <a href="reports.html" class="${getMenuClass('reports')}">
                                <i data-lucide="alert-circle" class="w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5"></i> 건의 / 신고함
                                <span id="badge-pending-reports" class="hidden ml-auto bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full shadow-sm">0건</span>
                            </a>
                        </li>
                        <li>
                            <a href="banners.html" class="${getMenuClass('banners')}">
                                <i data-lucide="image" class="w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5"></i> 메인 화면 광고 관리

                <div class="p-4 md:p-6 border-t border-gray-100 bg-gray-50 shrink-0">

}

// 2. 공통 헤더 컴포넌트
class AdminHeader extends HTMLElement {

        this.innerHTML = `

                    <div id="user-profile" class="hidden flex items-center gap-2 md:gap-3 border-l border-gray-200 pl-3 md:pl-6 cursor-pointer" onclick="logoutAdmin()">
                        <div class="w-8 h-8 md:w-10 md:h-10 rounded-full bg-rosegold-100 flex items-center justify-center text-rosegold-600 font-bold text-sm md:text-base">최</div>

                            <p class="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-gray-800">최고관리자</p>

                        </div>
                        <i data-lucide="log-out" class="w-4 h-4 md:w-4 md:h-4 text-gray-400 sm:hidden"></i>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </header>
        `;
    }
}

// 3. 공통 관리자 로그인 모달 컴포넌트
class AdminLoginModal extends HTMLElement {
    connectedCallback() {
        this.innerHTML = `
            <div id="modal-login" class="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[120] hidden flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-md">
                <div class="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-[2rem] md:rounded-[2.5rem] p-6 md:p-8 shadow-2xl relative animate-[fadeIn_0.3s_ease-out]">
                    <button onclick="closeLoginModal()" class="absolute top-5 right-5 md:top-6 md:right-6 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"><i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i></button>
                    <div class="text-center mb-6 md:mb-8 mt-2 md:mt-4">
                        <div class="w-14 h-14 md:w-16 md:h-16 bg-rosegold-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3 md:mb-4 text-rosegold-500">
                            <i data-lucide="heart-handshake" class="w-7 h-7 md:w-8 md:h-8"></i>
                        </div>
                        <h3 class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-gray-800 mb-1 md:mb-2">관리자 로그인</h3>
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium">이웃 관리 권한을 획득하세요.</p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="space-y-3 md:space-y-4">
                        <input type="text" id="login-id" placeholder="관리자 ID (admin)" class="w-full p-3 md:p-4 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-100 rounded-lg md:rounded-xl focus:border-rosegold-400 outline-none text-base md:text-lg font-bold">
                        <input type="password" id="login-pw" placeholder="비밀번호 (000000)" class="w-full p-3 md:p-4 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-100 rounded-lg md:rounded-xl focus:border-rosegold-400 outline-none text-base md:text-lg">
                        <button onclick="loginAdmin()" class="w-full bg-rosegold-500 text-white font-black text-lg md:text-xl py-4 md:py-5 rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl shadow-lg transition hover:bg-rosegold-600">시스템 접속하기</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    }
}

// 브라우저에 커스텀 태그 등록
customElements.define('admin-sidebar', AdminSidebar);
customElements.define('admin-header', AdminHeader);
customElements.define('admin-login-modal', AdminLoginModal);

// 모바일 사이드바 토글 로직 공통화
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('admin-sidebar');
    const overlay = document.getElementById('sidebar-overlay');
    const btnOpen = document.getElementById('btn-open-sidebar');
    const btnClose = document.getElementById('btn-close-sidebar');

    const toggleSidebar = () => {
        if(!sidebar || !overlay) return;
        const isClosed = sidebar.classList.contains('-translate-x-full');
        if (isClosed) {
            sidebar.classList.remove('-translate-x-full');
            overlay.classList.remove('hidden');
            setTimeout(() => overlay.classList.remove('opacity-0'), 10);
        } else {
            sidebar.classList.add('-translate-x-full');
            overlay.classList.add('opacity-0');
            setTimeout(() => overlay.classList.add('hidden'), 300);
        }
    };

    if(btnOpen) btnOpen.addEventListener('click', toggleSidebar);
    if(btnClose) btnClose.addEventListener('click', toggleSidebar);
    if(overlay) overlay.addEventListener('click', toggleSidebar);
    
    // 삽입된 HTML 요소들의 아이콘 활성화
    if(window.lucide) window.lucide.createIcons();
});

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ko">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>부산이음 - 관리사무소</title>
    <!-- Tailwind CSS -->
    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
    <!-- Lucide Icons -->
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/lucide@latest"></script>
    <!-- 웹 폰트 -->
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Noto+Sans+KR:wght@300;400;500;700;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
    <!-- 🌟 공통 메뉴/헤더/로그인 컴포넌트 불러오기 -->
    <script src="components.js"></script>

    <script>
        tailwind.config = {
            theme: {
                extend: {
                    fontFamily: {
                        sans: ['"Noto Sans KR"', 'sans-serif'],
                    },
                    colors: {
                        rosegold: {
                            50: '#fff0f0',
                            100: '#ffe4e6',
                            200: '#fecdd3',
                            300: '#fda4af',
                            400: '#fb7185',
                            500: '#e58e8e', 
                            600: '#d97777', 
                            700: '#be123c',
                            800: '#6c4a4a', 
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    </script>
    <style>
        /* 부드러운 스크롤바 */
        ::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 8px; height: 8px; }
        ::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: #f1f1f1; }
        ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: #e58e8e; border-radius: 10px; }
        ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover { background: #d97777; }
        .hide-scrollbar::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }
        .hide-scrollbar { -ms-overflow-style: none; scrollbar-width: none; }
    </style>
</head>
<body class="bg-[#F8F9FA] text-gray-800 font-sans antialiased h-screen flex overflow-hidden">

    <!-- 🌟 1. 공통 사이드바 컴포넌트 -->
    <admin-sidebar active="index"></admin-sidebar>

    <!-- 메인 콘텐츠 영역 -->
    <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col h-screen overflow-hidden w-full">
        
        <!-- 🌟 2. 공통 헤더 컴포넌트 -->
        <admin-header title="전체현황"></admin-header>

        <!-- 스크롤 가능한 메인 내용 -->
        <main class="flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-8">
            
            <!-- 상단 요약 카드 (3개로 조정, 모바일 최적화) -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 md:gap-5 mb-6 md:mb-8">
                <!-- 카드 1 (가입자 통계: 실시간 연동) -->
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-row sm:flex-col items-center sm:items-start justify-between hover:-translate-y-1 transition-transform">
                    <div class="flex sm:justify-between items-start mb-0 sm:mb-3">
                        <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-lg md:rounded-xl bg-blue-50 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-500 mr-4 sm:mr-0">
                            <i data-lucide="users" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                        </div>
                        <div class="sm:hidden flex flex-col justify-center">
                            <p class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-0.5">우리 동네 이웃</p>
                            <h3 id="stat-total-members-m" class="text-xl font-black text-gray-800 leading-none">0<span class="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold ml-1">명</span></h3>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="hidden sm:block">
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">우리 동네 이웃</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-total-members" class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-gray-800">0<span class="text-sm md:text-base text-gray-400 font-bold ml-1">명</span></h3>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- 카드 2 (동아리 통계: 실시간 연동) -->
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-row sm:flex-col items-center sm:items-start justify-between hover:-translate-y-1 transition-transform">
                    <div class="flex sm:justify-between items-start mb-0 sm:mb-3">
                        <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-lg md:rounded-xl bg-purple-50 flex items-center justify-center text-purple-500 mr-4 sm:mr-0">
                            <i data-lucide="camera" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                        </div>
                        <div class="sm:hidden flex flex-col justify-center">
                            <p class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-0.5">활동 중인 동아리</p>
                            <h3 id="stat-total-clubs-m" class="text-xl font-black text-gray-800 leading-none">0<span class="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold ml-1">개</span></h3>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="hidden sm:block">
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">활동 중인 동아리</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-total-clubs" class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-gray-800">0<span class="text-sm md:text-base text-gray-400 font-bold ml-1">개</span></h3>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- 카드 3 (소식 통계: 실시간 연동) -->
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6 shadow-md shadow-rosegold-100 border-2 border-rosegold-200 flex flex-row sm:flex-col items-center sm:items-start justify-between hover:-translate-y-1 transition-transform relative overflow-hidden">
                    <div class="absolute -right-4 -top-4 w-16 h-16 md:w-20 md:h-20 bg-rosegold-50 rounded-full z-0"></div>
                    <div class="flex sm:justify-between items-start mb-0 sm:mb-3 relative z-10">
                        <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-lg md:rounded-xl bg-rosegold-100 flex items-center justify-center text-rosegold-600 mr-4 sm:mr-0">
                            <i data-lucide="bell-ring" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                        </div>
                        <div class="sm:hidden flex flex-col justify-center">
                            <p class="text-xs font-bold text-rosegold-600 mb-0.5">전체 등록된 소식</p>
                            <h3 id="stat-total-posts-m" class="text-xl font-black text-rosegold-600 leading-none">0<span class="text-xs text-rosegold-400 font-bold ml-1">건</span></h3>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="relative z-10 hidden sm:block">
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-rosegold-600 mb-1">전체 등록된 소식</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-total-posts" class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-rosegold-600">0<span class="text-sm md:text-base text-rosegold-400 font-bold ml-1">건</span></h3>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 하단 상세 내역 영역 -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6 md:gap-8">
                
                <!-- 등록된 소식 권한 관리 (실시간 리스트 연동) -->
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
                    <div class="px-4 md:px-6 py-4 md:py-5 border-b border-gray-100 flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-50/50">
                        <div>
                            <h3 class="text-lg md:text-xl font-black text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-1.5 md:gap-2">
                                📝 등록된 소식 권한 관리
                            </h3>
                            <p class="hidden sm:block text-xs md:text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium mt-1">이웃들이 올린 소식을 확인하고 노출/숨김 권한을 설정하세요.</p>
                        </div>
                        <a href="market.html" class="px-2.5 py-1 md:px-3 md:py-1.5 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap">모두 보기</a>
                    </div>
                    
                    <!-- 🔥 JS로 리스트가 채워질 공간 🔥 -->
                    <div id="list-pending-posts" class="p-4 md:p-5 space-y-3 flex-1 overflow-y-auto max-h-[300px] md:max-h-[400px] hide-scrollbar">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-center h-32">
                            <span class="text-sm md:text-base text-gray-400 font-medium">관리자 로그인이 필요합니다.</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- 최근 가입 회원 목록 (실시간 리스트 연동) -->
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
                    <div class="px-4 md:px-6 py-4 md:py-5 border-b border-gray-100 flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-50/50">
                        <div>
                            <h3 class="text-lg md:text-xl font-black text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-1.5 md:gap-2">
                                👋 새로 이사 온 이웃들
                            </h3>
                            <p class="hidden sm:block text-xs md:text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium mt-1">최근에 부산이음에 가입한 분들입니다.</p>
                        </div>
                        <a href="members.html" class="px-2.5 py-1 md:px-3 md:py-1.5 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap">모두 보기</a>
                    </div>
                    
                    <!-- 🔥 JS로 리스트가 채워질 공간 🔥 -->
                    <div id="list-recent-members" class="p-4 md:p-5 space-y-2 flex-1 overflow-y-auto max-h-[300px] md:max-h-[400px] hide-scrollbar">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-center h-32">
                            <span class="text-sm md:text-base text-gray-400 font-medium">관리자 로그인이 필요합니다.</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

            </div>
        </main>
    </div>

    <!-- 🌟 3. 공통 로그인 모달 컴포넌트 -->
    <admin-login-modal></admin-login-modal>

    <script>
        // Lucide 아이콘 초기화
        lucide.createIcons();
    </script>

    <!-- 🔥 파이어베이스 실시간 데이터 연동 (관리사무소 첫 화면) 🔥 -->
    <script type="module">
        import { db, auth, appId } from '../firebase-config.js';
        import { collection, onSnapshot, updateDoc, doc, collectionGroup } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js";
        import { onAuthStateChanged, signInWithEmailAndPassword, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js";

        // UI 함수
        window.openLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.remove('hidden');
        window.closeLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.add('hidden');

        // 관리자 로그아웃 추가
        window.logoutAdmin = () => {
            if(confirm("관리자 계정에서 로그아웃 하시겠습니까?")) {
                signOut(auth).then(() => location.reload());
            }
        }

        // 🌟 에러 없이 깔끔한 관리자 전용 로그인!
        window.loginAdmin = () => {
            const id = document.getElementById('login-id').value;
            const pw = document.getElementById('login-pw').value;
            if (id === 'admin') {
                signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, 'admin@internal.system', pw).then(() => { 
                    closeLoginModal(); 
                }).catch(() => alert('비밀번호 오류! 파이어베이스 콘솔에 계정을 만들었는지 확인해주세요.'));
            } else { 
                alert('ID를 확인해주세요. (admin)'); 
            }
        };

        // 인증 상태 감지 (관리자 접속 시에만 데이터 로드)
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
            const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login');
            const prof = document.getElementById('user-profile');

            if (user && user.email === 'admin@internal.system') {
                // 최고관리자 로그인 성공! 버튼 숨기고 프로필 노출
                if(btn) btn.classList.add('hidden');
                if(prof) { prof.classList.remove('hidden'); prof.classList.add('flex'); }

                // --- 1. 소식 (우리기장) 연동 ---
                const marketRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market');
                onSnapshot(marketRef, (snapshot) => {
                    const posts = [];
                    let pendingCount = 0;

                    snapshot.forEach(doc => {
                        const data = doc.data();
                        posts.push({ id: doc.id, ...data });
                        if(data.status === 'pending') pendingCount++;
                    });

                    const badge = document.getElementById('badge-pending-posts');
                    if (badge) {
                        if(pendingCount > 0) { 
                            badge.innerText = pendingCount + '건'; 
                            badge.classList.remove('hidden'); 
                        } else { 
                            badge.classList.add('hidden'); 
                        }
                    }

                    posts.sort((a, b) => {
                        const tA = (a.createdAt && typeof a.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? a.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                        const tB = (b.createdAt && typeof b.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? b.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                        return tB - tA; 
                    });

                    // PC/모바일 통계 텍스트 업데이트
                    const statTotalPosts = document.getElementById('stat-total-posts');
                    const statTotalPostsM = document.getElementById('stat-total-posts-m');
                    if(statTotalPosts) statTotalPosts.innerHTML = `${posts.length}<span class="text-sm md:text-base text-rosegold-400 font-bold ml-1">건</span>`;
                    if(statTotalPostsM) statTotalPostsM.innerHTML = `${posts.length}<span class="text-xs text-rosegold-400 font-bold ml-1">건</span>`;

                    const pendingContainer = document.getElementById('list-pending-posts');
                    if (posts.length === 0) {
                        pendingContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="flex items-center justify-center h-full text-sm md:text-base text-gray-400 font-medium">아직 등록된 소식이 없습니다.</div>';
                    } else {
                        pendingContainer.innerHTML = posts.map(post => {
                            let icon = 'image'; let categoryName = post.category;
                            if(post.category === 'cafe') { icon = 'coffee'; categoryName = '카페 추천'; }
                            if(post.category === 'food') { icon = 'utensils'; categoryName = '맛집 추천'; }
                            if(post.category === 'tour') { icon = 'map'; categoryName = '관광 명소'; }
                            if(post.category === 'club') { icon = 'users'; categoryName = '동아리 모집'; }

                            const isHidden = post.status === 'hidden';
                            const statusBadge = isHidden 
                                ? '<span class="text-[9px] md:text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 bg-gray-100 px-1.5 md:px-2 py-0.5 rounded mb-1 inline-block border border-gray-200">숨김 상태</span>'
                                : '<span class="text-[9px] md:text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 px-1.5 md:px-2 py-0.5 rounded mb-1 inline-block border border-emerald-100">노출 중</span>';

                            const actionButtons = isHidden
                                ? `<button onclick="updatePostStatus('${post.id}', 'active')" class="w-full sm:w-auto px-3 md:px-4 py-1.5 md:py-2 bg-rosegold-500 text-white font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-rosegold-600 transition shadow-sm text-xs md:text-sm">노출 권한 부여</button>`
                                : `<button onclick="updatePostStatus('${post.id}', 'hidden')" class="w-full sm:w-auto px-3 md:px-4 py-1.5 md:py-2 bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition text-xs md:text-sm border border-gray-200">숨김 처리</button>`;

                            return `
                            <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between p-3 md:p-4 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl shadow-sm hover:border-rosegold-300 transition gap-3 ${isHidden ? 'opacity-60 bg-gray-50' : ''}">
                                <div class="flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4 w-full sm:w-auto overflow-hidden">
                                    <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-lg bg-gray-50 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0 border border-gray-100">
                                        <i data-lucide="${icon}" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="min-w-0">
                                        ${statusBadge}
                                        <span class="text-[9px] md:text-[10px] font-bold text-yellow-600 bg-yellow-50 px-1.5 md:px-2 py-0.5 rounded mb-1 inline-block ml-1">${categoryName}</span>
                                        <h4 class="font-bold text-sm md:text-base text-gray-800 mb-0.5 truncate w-[180px] sm:w-[150px] md:w-[240px]">${post.title}</h4>
                                        <p class="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">작성자: <span class="font-bold text-gray-700">${post.authorName || '따뜻한 이웃'}</span></p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="flex w-full sm:w-auto flex-shrink-0">
                                    ${actionButtons}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            `;
                        }).join('');
                        lucide.createIcons();
                    }
                }, (error) => console.error("우리기장 데이터 가져오기 실패:", error));

                // --- 2. 가입한 이웃(회원) 실시간 동기화 ---
                const membersRef = collectionGroup(db, 'profile');
                onSnapshot(membersRef, (snapshot) => {
                    const members = [];
                    snapshot.forEach(doc => members.push({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
                    
                    members.sort((a, b) => {
                        const tA = (a.createdAt && typeof a.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? a.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                        const tB = (b.createdAt && typeof b.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? b.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                        return tB - tA;
                    });

                    // PC/모바일 통계 텍스트 업데이트
                    const statTotalMembers = document.getElementById('stat-total-members');
                    const statTotalMembersM = document.getElementById('stat-total-members-m');
                    if(statTotalMembers) statTotalMembers.innerHTML = `${members.length}<span class="text-sm md:text-base text-gray-400 font-bold ml-1">명</span>`;
                    if(statTotalMembersM) statTotalMembersM.innerHTML = `${members.length}<span class="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold ml-1">명</span>`;

                    const membersContainer = document.getElementById('list-recent-members');
                    if(members.length === 0) {
                        membersContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="flex items-center justify-center h-full text-sm md:text-base text-gray-400 font-medium">아직 가입한 이웃이 없습니다.</div>';
                    } else {
                        membersContainer.innerHTML = members.slice(0, 5).map(member => {
                            const initial = member.name ? member.name.charAt(0) : '?';
                            return `
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-2 md:p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-50 transition border border-transparent hover:border-gray-100">
                                <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 md:gap-3">
                                    <div class="w-8 h-8 md:w-10 md:h-10 rounded-full bg-blue-100 flex items-center justify-center text-sm md:text-base font-black text-blue-600">${initial}</div>
                                    <div>
                                        <p class="text-sm md:text-base font-bold text-gray-800 truncate max-w-[120px] md:max-w-[200px]">${member.name || '이름 없음'} <span class="text-[10px] md:text-xs font-normal text-gray-500 ml-0.5 md:ml-1">(${member.nickname || '별명 없음'})</span></p>
                                        <p class="text-[10px] md:text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 mt-0.5 truncate max-w-[120px] md:max-w-[200px]">${member.email || ''}</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <span class="px-2 md:px-3 py-0.5 md:py-1 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 border border-emerald-200 rounded-full text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap">신규 가입</span>
                            </div>
                            `;
                        }).join('');
                    }
                }, (error) => console.error("회원 데이터 가져오기 실패:", error));

                // --- 3. 동아리 개수 실시간 동기화 ---
                const clubsRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs');
                onSnapshot(clubsRef, (snapshot) => {
                    const statTotalClubs = document.getElementById('stat-total-clubs');
                    const statTotalClubsM = document.getElementById('stat-total-clubs-m');
                    if(statTotalClubs) statTotalClubs.innerHTML = `${snapshot.size}<span class="text-sm md:text-base text-gray-400 font-bold ml-1">개</span>`;
                    if(statTotalClubsM) statTotalClubsM.innerHTML = `${snapshot.size}<span class="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold ml-1">개</span>`;
                }, console.error);
                
                // --- 4. 기타 알림 배지 (건의/신고) 동기화 ---
                const reportsRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'reports');
                onSnapshot(reportsRef, (snap) => {
                    let count = 0; snap.forEach(d => { if(d.data().status === 'pending') count++; });
                    const b = document.getElementById('badge-pending-reports');
                    if(b) { if(count > 0) { b.innerText = count+'건'; b.classList.remove('hidden'); } else b.classList.add('hidden'); }
                });

            } else {
                // 비로그인 상태이거나 관리자가 아닐 때 -> 로그인 창 강제 팝업!
                const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login');
                const prof = document.getElementById('user-profile');
                if(btn) btn.classList.remove('hidden');
                if(prof) prof.classList.add('hidden');

                const pendingList = document.getElementById('list-pending-posts');
                if(pendingList) pendingList.innerHTML = '<div class="flex items-center justify-center h-full text-sm md:text-base text-gray-400 font-medium">관리자 로그인이 필요합니다.</div>';
                
                const memberList = document.getElementById('list-recent-members');
                if(memberList) memberList.innerHTML = '<div class="flex items-center justify-center h-full text-sm md:text-base text-gray-400 font-medium">관리자 로그인이 필요합니다.</div>';
                
                // 🌟 관리자 페이지 접속 시 자동으로 로그인 창 띄우기
                openLoginModal();
            }
        });

        // 💡 전역 함수: 게시글 권한 상태 변경 (노출/숨김)
        window.updatePostStatus = async (postId, newStatus) => {
            try {
                await updateDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market', postId), {
                    status: newStatus
                });
                alert(newStatus === 'active' 
                    ? '✅ 정상적으로 사이트에 노출됩니다.' 
                    : '🚫 관리자 권한으로 게시글이 숨김 처리되었습니다.'
                );
            } catch(e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert('상태 변경에 실패했습니다. 관리자 권한을 다시 확인해주세요.');
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

    <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col h-screen overflow-hidden w-full">

        <main class="flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto bg-gray-50 p-4 md:p-8">
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 md:gap-6 mb-6 md:mb-8">
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-4 md:p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-full bg-gray-100 flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500 flex-shrink-0">
