                    <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-full bg-yellow-50 flex items-center justify-center text-yellow-500 flex-shrink-0">
                        <i data-lucide="alert-circle" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium">노출 승인 대기</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-pending-posts" class="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">0</h3>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-4 md:p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-500 flex-shrink-0">
                        <i data-lucide="check-circle" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium">현재 노출 중</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-active-posts" class="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">0</h3>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4 mb-6">
                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 w-full lg:w-auto">
                    <select class="flex-1 sm:flex-none px-3 md:px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-700 font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-rosegold-300 shadow-sm cursor-pointer">
                        <option value="all">전체 상태</option>
                        <option value="pending">승인 대기</option>
                        <option value="active">노출 중</option>
                        <option value="hidden">숨김</option>
                    </select>

                    <div class="relative w-full sm:w-auto mt-2 sm:mt-0">
                        <i data-lucide="search" class="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400"></i>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="제목, 내용 검색" class="pl-9 pr-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-rosegold-300 shadow-sm w-full sm:w-64 md:w-80 transition-all">
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 소식 목록 테이블 영역 -->
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
                <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                    <table class="w-full text-left border-collapse whitespace-nowrap">
                        <thead>
                            <tr class="bg-gray-50 text-gray-500 text-sm border-b border-gray-100">
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium w-12 text-center">
                                    <input type="checkbox" id="check-all" onchange="toggleAll(this)" class="rounded border-gray-300 text-rosegold-500 focus:ring-rosegold-500 cursor-pointer">
                                </th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium w-24 text-center">분류</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">제목 및 내용</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">작성자</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">등록일</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-center">상태</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-center">관리</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody id="market-list-body" class="text-sm text-gray-700">
                            <tr>
                                <td colspan="7" class="px-6 py-12 text-center text-gray-500 font-medium">
                                    데이터를 불러오는 중...
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <button onclick="activateSelected()" class="flex-1 sm:flex-none px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 text-emerald-600 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-50 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap">선택 노출 승인</button>
                <button onclick="hideSelected()" class="flex-1 sm:flex-none px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap">선택 숨김 처리</button>
                <button onclick="deleteSelected()" class="w-full sm:w-auto px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-red-200 text-red-600 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap mt-2 sm:mt-0">선택 삭제</button>
            </div>
        </main>
    </div>

    <!-- 🌟 3. 공통 로그인 모달 컴포넌트 -->
    <admin-login-modal></admin-login-modal>

    <script>
        lucide.createIcons();
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        import { db, auth, appId } from '../firebase-config.js';
        import { collection, onSnapshot, updateDoc, doc, deleteDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js";
        import { onAuthStateChanged, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js";

        window.openLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.remove('hidden');
        window.closeLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.add('hidden');

        window.logoutAdmin = () => {
            if(confirm("관리자 계정에서 로그아웃 하시겠습니까?")) {
                signOut(auth).then(() => location.reload());
            }
        };

        window.toggleAll = (source) => {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.post-checkbox');
            checkboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = source.checked);
        };

        const getSelectedIds = () => {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.post-checkbox:checked');
            return Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => cb.value);
        };

        window.activateSelected = async () => {
            const ids = getSelectedIds();
            if (ids.length === 0) return alert("활성화할 소식을 먼저 선택해주세요.");
            if (!confirm(`선택한 ${ids.length}개의 소식을 '노출 중' 상태로 일괄 변경하시겠습니까?`)) return;

            try {
                for (const id of ids) {
                    await updateDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market', id), { status: 'active' });
                }
                alert("✅ 노출 승인 처리가 완료되었습니다.");
                document.getElementById('check-all').checked = false;
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert("❌ 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다. 관리자 권한을 확인해주세요.");
            }
        };

        window.hideSelected = async () => {
            const ids = getSelectedIds();
            if (ids.length === 0) return alert("숨김 처리할 소식을 먼저 선택해주세요.");
            if (!confirm(`선택한 ${ids.length}개의 소식을 숨김 처리하시겠습니까?`)) return;

            try {
                for (const id of ids) {
                    await updateDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market', id), { status: 'hidden' });
                }
                alert("✅ 숨김 처리가 완료되었습니다.");
                document.getElementById('check-all').checked = false;
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert("❌ 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.");
            }
        };

        window.deleteSelected = async () => {
            const ids = getSelectedIds();
            if (ids.length === 0) return alert("삭제할 소식을 먼저 선택해주세요.");
            if (!confirm(`경고: 선택한 ${ids.length}개의 소식 데이터를 완전히 삭제합니다. 진행할까요?`)) return;

            try {
                for (const id of ids) {
                    await deleteDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market', id));
                }
                alert("🗑️ 삭제 처리가 완료되었습니다.");
                document.getElementById('check-all').checked = false;
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert("❌ 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.");
            }
        };

        onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('market-list-body');
            if (user && user.email === 'admin@internal.system') {
                startListening();
            } else {
                if(tbody) tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-6 py-20 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium italic">관리자 로그인이 필요합니다. 상단의 로그인 버튼을 눌러주세요.</td></tr>';
                openLoginModal();
            }
        });

        const startListening = () => {
            const marketRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market');
            onSnapshot(marketRef, (snapshot) => {
                const posts = [];
                let pendingCount = 0;
                let activeCount = 0;

                snapshot.forEach(docSnap => { 
                    const data = docSnap.data();
                    posts.push({ id: docSnap.id, ...data }); 
                    if(data.status === 'pending') pendingCount++;
                    if(data.status === 'active') activeCount++;
                });

                posts.sort((a, b) => {
                    if (a.status === 'pending' && b.status !== 'pending') return -1;
                    if (a.status !== 'pending' && b.status === 'pending') return 1;
                    const tA = (a.createdAt && typeof a.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? a.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                    const tB = (b.createdAt && typeof b.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? b.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                    return tB - tA;
                });

                const statTotal = document.getElementById('stat-total-posts');
                const statPending = document.getElementById('stat-pending-posts');
                const statActive = document.getElementById('stat-active-posts');
                if(statTotal) statTotal.innerText = snapshot.size.toLocaleString();
                if(statPending) statPending.innerText = pendingCount.toLocaleString();
                if(statActive) statActive.innerText = activeCount.toLocaleString();

                const tbody = document.getElementById('market-list-body');
                if (!tbody) return;

                if (posts.length === 0) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-6 py-12 text-center text-gray-500 font-medium">아직 등록된 동네 소식이 없습니다.</td></tr>`;
                } else {
                    tbody.innerHTML = posts.map(post => {
                        let icon = 'image'; let categoryName = post.category;
                        if(post.category === 'cafe') { icon = 'coffee'; categoryName = '카페 추천'; }
                        if(post.category === 'food') { icon = 'utensils'; categoryName = '맛집 추천'; }
                        if(post.category === 'tour') { icon = 'map'; categoryName = '관광 명소'; }
                        if(post.category === 'club') { icon = 'users'; categoryName = '동아리 모집'; }

                        let dateStr = '-';
                        if (post.createdAt && typeof post.createdAt.toDate === 'function') {
                            const d = post.createdAt.toDate();
                            dateStr = `${d.getFullYear()}.${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}.${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
                        }

                        let statusBadge = '<span class="px-2 md:px-2.5 py-0.5 md:py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-full text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap">노출 중</span>';
                        if (post.status === 'pending') statusBadge = '<span class="px-2 md:px-2.5 py-0.5 md:py-1 bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700 rounded-full text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap">승인 대기</span>';
                        else if (post.status === 'hidden') statusBadge = '<span class="px-2 md:px-2.5 py-0.5 md:py-1 bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 rounded-full text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap">숨김</span>';

                        const actionButtons = post.status === 'hidden' || post.status === 'pending'
                                ? `<button onclick="updatePostStatus('${post.id}', 'active')" class="w-full sm:w-auto px-3 md:px-4 py-1.5 md:py-2 bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-600 transition shadow-sm text-xs md:text-sm whitespace-nowrap">승인(노출)</button>`
                                : `<button onclick="updatePostStatus('${post.id}', 'hidden')" class="w-full sm:w-auto px-3 md:px-4 py-1.5 md:py-2 bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition text-xs md:text-sm border border-gray-200 whitespace-nowrap">숨기기</button>`;

                        return `
                            <tr class="border-b border-gray-50 hover:bg-rosegold-50/30 transition ${post.status === 'hidden' ? 'opacity-60 bg-gray-50' : ''}">
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center">
                                    <input type="checkbox" value="${post.id}" class="post-checkbox rounded border-gray-300 text-rosegold-500 focus:ring-rosegold-500 cursor-pointer">
                                </td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center">
                                    <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-lg bg-gray-50 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400 border border-gray-100 mx-auto">
                                        <i data-lucide="${icon}" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                                    </div>
                                    <p class="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 mt-1">${categoryName}</p>
                                </td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4">
                                    <p class="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm md:text-base truncate max-w-[150px] sm:max-w-[200px] md:max-w-[300px] mb-0.5" title="${post.title}">${post.title}</p>
                                    <p class="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-gray-400 truncate max-w-[150px] sm:max-w-[200px] md:max-w-[300px]">${post.content}</p>
                                </td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-gray-600 text-xs md:text-sm whitespace-nowrap">${post.authorName || '이웃'}</td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-gray-500 text-[10px] md:text-xs whitespace-nowrap">${dateStr}</td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center">${statusBadge}</td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center">
                                    <div class="flex justify-center gap-2">
                                        ${actionButtons}
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        `;
                    }).join('');
                    lucide.createIcons();
                }
            }, (error) => console.error("우리기장 데이터 가져오기 실패:", error));
        };

        window.updatePostStatus = async (postId, newStatus) => {
            try {
                await updateDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market', postId), { status: newStatus });
            } catch(e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert('상태 변경에 실패했습니다. 관리자 권한을 다시 확인해주세요.');
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

<body class="bg-gray-50 text-gray-800 font-sans antialiased h-screen flex overflow-hidden">

    <!-- 🌟 1. 공통 사이드바 컴포넌트 -->

    <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col h-screen overflow-hidden w-full">

        <admin-header title="이웃(회원) 관리"></admin-header>

        <main class="flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto bg-gray-50 p-4 md:p-8">
            <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4 mb-6">
                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 w-full lg:w-auto">
                    <select class="flex-1 sm:flex-none px-3 md:px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-700 font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-rosegold-300 shadow-sm cursor-pointer">
                        <option value="all">전체 상태</option>
                        <option value="active">정상 회원</option>
                        <option value="suspended">정지 회원</option>
                    </select>
                    
                    <select class="flex-1 sm:flex-none px-3 md:px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-700 font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-rosegold-300 shadow-sm cursor-pointer">
                        <option value="all">전체 기간</option>
                        <option value="today">오늘 가입</option>
                        <option value="week">최근 1주일</option>
                    </select>

                    <div class="relative w-full sm:w-auto mt-2 sm:mt-0">
                        <i data-lucide="search" class="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400"></i>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="이름, 닉네임 검색" class="pl-9 pr-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-rosegold-300 shadow-sm w-full sm:w-64 md:w-80 transition-all">
                    </div>
                </div>

                <button class="w-full lg:w-auto px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 hover:text-gray-800 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm mt-2 lg:mt-0">
                    <i data-lucide="download" class="w-4 h-4"></i>회원목록 엑셀 다운로드
                </button>
            </div>

            <!-- 회원 목록 테이블 영역 -->
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
                <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                    <table class="w-full text-left border-collapse whitespace-nowrap">
                        <thead>
                            <tr class="bg-gray-50 text-gray-500 text-sm border-b border-gray-100">
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium w-12 text-center">
                                    <input type="checkbox" id="check-all" onchange="toggleAll(this)" class="rounded border-gray-300 text-rosegold-500 focus:ring-rosegold-500 cursor-pointer">
                                </th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">이름(닉네임)</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">연락처</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">참여 동아리</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">가입일</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-center">상태</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-center">관리</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody id="member-list-body" class="text-sm text-gray-700">
                            <tr>
                                <td colspan="7" class="px-6 py-12 text-center text-gray-500 font-medium">
                                    데이터를 불러오는 중...
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
                
                <div class="px-6 py-4 border-t border-gray-100 bg-white flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4">
                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-500">전체 <span id="stat-total-bottom" class="font-bold text-gray-800">0</span>명 중 1-10</p>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                        <button class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-50 transition" disabled><i data-lucide="chevron-left" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
                        <button class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-rosegold-500 text-white font-bold transition">1</button>
                        <button class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 transition">2</button>
                        <button class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 transition">3</button>
                        <button class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 transition"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <button onclick="suspendSelected()" class="flex-1 sm:flex-none px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap">선택 회원 정지</button>
                <button onclick="deleteSelected()" class="w-full sm:w-auto px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-red-200 text-red-600 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap mt-2 sm:mt-0">선택 회원 삭제</button>
            </div>
        </main>
    </div>

    <!-- 🌟 3. 공통 로그인 모달 컴포넌트 -->
    <admin-login-modal></admin-login-modal>

    <script>
        lucide.createIcons();
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        import { db, auth, appId } from '../firebase-config.js';
        import { collectionGroup, onSnapshot, collection, updateDoc, doc, deleteDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js";
        import { onAuthStateChanged, signInWithEmailAndPassword, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js";

        window.openLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.remove('hidden');
        window.closeLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.add('hidden');

        window.logoutAdmin = () => {
            if(confirm("관리자 계정에서 로그아웃 하시겠습니까?")) {
                signOut(auth).then(() => location.reload());
            }
        };

        window.loginAdmin = () => {
            const id = document.getElementById('login-id').value;
            const pw = document.getElementById('login-pw').value;
            if (id === 'admin') {
                signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, 'admin@internal.system', pw).then(() => { 
                    closeLoginModal(); 
                }).catch(() => alert('비밀번호가 올바르지 않습니다.'));
            } else { 
                alert('ID 확인 (admin)'); 
            }
        };

        window.toggleAll = (source) => {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.member-checkbox');
            checkboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = source.checked);
        };

        const getSelectedIds = () => {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.member-checkbox:checked');
            return Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => cb.value);
        };

        window.suspendSelected = async () => {
            const ids = getSelectedIds();
            if (ids.length === 0) return alert("정지할 회원을 먼저 선택해주세요.");
            if (!confirm(`선택한 ${ids.length}명의 회원을 정말 '정지' 처리하시겠습니까?`)) return;

            try {
                for (const uid of ids) {
                    await updateDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', uid, 'profile', 'info'), { status: 'suspended' });
                }
                alert("✅ 정지 처리가 완료되었습니다.");
                document.getElementById('check-all').checked = false;
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert("❌ 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다. 관리자 권한을 확인해주세요.");
            }
        };

        window.deleteSelected = async () => {
            const ids = getSelectedIds();
            if (ids.length === 0) return alert("삭제할 회원을 먼저 선택해주세요.");
            if (!confirm(`경고: 선택한 ${ids.length}명의 회원 정보를 완전히 삭제합니다.\n이 작업은 되돌릴 수 없습니다. 진행할까요?`)) return;

            try {
                for (const uid of ids) {
                    await deleteDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', uid, 'profile', 'info'));
                }
                alert("🗑️ 삭제 처리가 완료되었습니다.");
                document.getElementById('check-all').checked = false;
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
                alert("❌ 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다. 관리자 권한을 확인해주세요.");
            }
        };

        onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
            const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login');
            const prof = document.getElementById('user-profile');
            const tbody = document.getElementById('member-list-body');

            if (user && user.email === 'admin@internal.system') {
                if(btn) btn.classList.add('hidden'); 
                if(prof) { prof.classList.remove('hidden'); prof.classList.add('flex'); }
                startListening();
            } else {
                if(btn) btn.classList.remove('hidden'); 
                if(prof) prof.classList.add('hidden');
                
                if(tbody) tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-6 py-20 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium italic">관리자 로그인이 필요합니다. 상단의 로그인 버튼을 눌러주세요.</td></tr>';
                openLoginModal();
            }
        });

        const startListening = () => {
            onSnapshot(collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market'), (snap) => {
                let count = 0; snap.forEach(d => { if(d.data().status === 'pending') count++; });
                const b = document.getElementById('badge-pending-posts');
                if(b) { if(count > 0) { b.innerText = count+'건'; b.classList.remove('hidden'); } else b.classList.add('hidden'); }
            });
            onSnapshot(collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'reports'), (snap) => {
                let count = 0; snap.forEach(d => { if(d.data().status === 'pending') count++; });
                const b = document.getElementById('badge-pending-reports');
                if(b) { if(count > 0) { b.innerText = count+'건'; b.classList.remove('hidden'); } else b.classList.add('hidden'); }
            });

            const membersRef = collectionGroup(db, 'profile');
            onSnapshot(membersRef, (snapshot) => {
                const members = [];
                snapshot.forEach(docSnap => { 
                    const pathSegments = docSnap.ref.path.split('/');
                    const userId = pathSegments[pathSegments.length - 3];
                    members.push({ id: userId, ...docSnap.data() }); 
                });

                members.sort((a, b) => {
                    const tA = (a.createdAt && typeof a.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? a.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                    const tB = (b.createdAt && typeof b.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? b.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                    return tB - tA;
                });

                const statBottom = document.getElementById('stat-total-bottom');
                if(statBottom) statBottom.innerText = members.length.toLocaleString();

                const tbody = document.getElementById('member-list-body');
                if (!tbody) return;

                if (members.length === 0) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-6 py-12 text-center text-gray-500 font-medium">현재 가입한 회원이 없습니다.</td></tr>`;
                } else {
                    const colorMap = [
                        { bg: 'bg-blue-100', text: 'text-blue-600' }, { bg: 'bg-purple-100', text: 'text-purple-600' },
                        { bg: 'bg-emerald-100', text: 'text-emerald-600' }, { bg: 'bg-yellow-100', text: 'text-yellow-600' },
                        { bg: 'bg-gray-200', text: 'text-gray-600' }
                    ];

                    tbody.innerHTML = members.map(member => {
                        const initial = member.name ? member.name.charAt(0) : '?';
                        const colorClass = colorMap[initial.charCodeAt(0) % colorMap.length] || colorMap[0];
                        
                        let dateStr = '-';
                        if (member.createdAt && typeof member.createdAt.toDate === 'function') {
                            const d = member.createdAt.toDate();
                            dateStr = `${d.getFullYear()}.${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}.${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
                        }

                        let statusBadge = '<span class="px-2 md:px-2.5 py-0.5 md:py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-full text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap">정상</span>';
                        if (member.status === 'suspended') statusBadge = '<span class="px-2 md:px-2.5 py-0.5 md:py-1 bg-red-100 text-red-700 rounded-full text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap">정지</span>';
                        else if (member.status === 'withdrawn') statusBadge = '<span class="px-2 md:px-2.5 py-0.5 md:py-1 bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 rounded-full text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap">탈퇴</span>';

                        return `
                            <tr class="border-b border-gray-50 hover:bg-rosegold-50/30 transition">
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center">
                                    <input type="checkbox" value="${member.id}" class="member-checkbox rounded border-gray-300 text-rosegold-500 focus:ring-rosegold-500 cursor-pointer">
                                </td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4">
                                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 md:gap-3">
                                        <div class="w-8 h-8 md:w-10 md:h-10 rounded-full ${colorClass.bg} flex items-center justify-center text-xs md:text-sm font-bold ${colorClass.text} flex-shrink-0">${initial}</div>
                                        <div>
                                            <p class="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm md:text-base truncate max-w-[100px] sm:max-w-[150px] md:max-w-none">${member.name || '이름없음'} <span class="font-normal text-gray-500 text-[10px] md:text-xs ml-1">(${member.nickname || '별명없음'})</span></p>
                                            <p class="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-gray-400 truncate max-w-[100px] sm:max-w-[150px] md:max-w-none">${member.email || '-'}</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-gray-600 text-xs md:text-sm whitespace-nowrap">${member.phone || '-'}</td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-gray-600 text-xs md:text-sm whitespace-nowrap">-</td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-gray-500 text-[10px] md:text-xs whitespace-nowrap">${dateStr}</td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center">${statusBadge}</td>
                                <td class="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 text-center">
                                    <button class="text-gray-400 hover:text-rosegold-600 transition" title="상세보기/수정"><i data-lucide="edit" class="w-4 h-4 md:w-5 md:h-5 mx-auto"></i></button>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        `;
                    }).join('');
                    lucide.createIcons();
                }
            }, (error) => console.error("회원 데이터 가져오기 실패 (인덱스 에러 가능성):", error));
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

<body class="bg-gray-50 text-gray-800 font-sans antialiased h-screen flex overflow-hidden">

    <!-- 🌟 1. 공통 사이드바 컴포넌트 -->
    <admin-sidebar active="reports"></admin-sidebar>

    <!-- 메인 콘텐츠 영역 -->
    <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col h-screen overflow-hidden w-full">
        
        <!-- 🌟 2. 공통 헤더 컴포넌트 -->
        <admin-header title="건의 / 신고함"></admin-header>

        <!-- 스크롤 가능한 메인 내용 -->
        <main class="flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto bg-gray-50 p-4 md:p-8">
            
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 md:gap-6 mb-6 md:mb-8">
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-4 md:p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-full bg-rosegold-50 flex items-center justify-center text-rosegold-500 flex-shrink-0">
                        <i data-lucide="message-square" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium">전체 문의/신고</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-total-reports" class="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">0</h3>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-4 md:p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-full bg-yellow-50 flex items-center justify-center text-yellow-500 flex-shrink-0">
                        <i data-lucide="help-circle" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium">미처리 내역</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-pending-reports" class="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">0</h3>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-4 md:p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-full bg-red-50 flex items-center justify-center text-red-500 flex-shrink-0">
                        <i data-lucide="alert-triangle" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium">신규 신고</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-new-reports" class="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">0</h3>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="bg-white rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-4 md:p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div class="w-10 h-10 md:w-12 md:h-12 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-500 flex-shrink-0">
                        <i data-lucide="check-circle" class="w-5 h-5 md:w-6 md:h-6"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <p class="text-xs md:text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium">처리 완료</p>
                        <h3 id="stat-resolved-reports" class="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">0</h3>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 필터 및 검색 영역 -->
            <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4 mb-6">
                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                    <select class="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-700 font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-rosegold-300 shadow-sm cursor-pointer">
                        <option value="all">전체 유형</option>
                        <option value="inquiry">일반 문의</option>
                        <option value="report">신고 접수</option>
                    </select>
                    
                    <select class="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-700 font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-rosegold-300 shadow-sm cursor-pointer">
                        <option value="all">전체 상태</option>
                        <option value="pending">대기 중 (미처리)</option>
                        <option value="resolved">처리 완료</option>
                    </select>

                    <div class="relative w-full md:w-auto">
                        <i data-lucide="search" class="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400"></i>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="제목, 작성자 검색" class="pl-9 pr-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-rosegold-300 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-rosegold-300 shadow-sm w-full md:w-80 transition-all">
                    </div>
                </div>

                <button class="w-full md:w-auto px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm">
                    <i data-lucide="download" class="w-4 h-4"></i> 엑셀 다운로드
                </button>
            </div>

            <!-- 문의/신고 목록 테이블 영역 -->
            <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
                <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                    <table class="w-full text-left border-collapse whitespace-nowrap">
                        <thead>
                            <tr class="bg-gray-50 text-gray-500 text-sm border-b border-gray-100">
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium w-12 text-center"><input type="checkbox" class="rounded border-gray-300 text-rosegold-500 focus:ring-rosegold-500"></th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-center w-24">유형</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">제목 / 내용</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">작성자</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">작성일</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium">상태</th>
                                <th class="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-center">관리</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody id="report-list-body" class="text-sm text-gray-700">
                            <tr>
                                <td colspan="7" class="px-6 py-12 text-center text-gray-500 font-medium">
                                    <div class="animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-b-2 border-rosegold-500 mx-auto"></div>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
                
                <div class="px-6 py-4 border-t border-gray-100 bg-white flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4">
                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-500">전체 <span id="stat-total-bottom" class="font-bold text-gray-800">0</span>개 중 1-10</p>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
                        <button class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-50 transition" disabled><i data-lucide="chevron-left" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
                        <button class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-rosegold-500 text-white font-bold transition">1</button>
                        <button class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 transition">2</button>
                        <button class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 transition">3</button>
                        <button class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 transition"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-4 h-4"></i></button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <button class="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-200 text-emerald-600 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-50 transition shadow-sm">선택 일괄 처리 완료</button>
                <button class="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-red-200 text-red-600 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50 transition shadow-sm">선택 삭제</button>
            </div>

        </main>
    </div>

    <!-- 🌟 3. 공통 로그인 모달 컴포넌트 -->
    <admin-login-modal></admin-login-modal>

    <script>
        lucide.createIcons();
    </script>

    <!-- 🔥 파이어베이스 실시간 데이터 연동 (문의/신고 관리) 🔥 -->
    <script type="module">
        import { db, auth, appId } from '../firebase-config.js';
        import { collection, onSnapshot, updateDoc, doc, deleteDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js";
        import { onAuthStateChanged, signInWithEmailAndPassword, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js";

        window.openLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.remove('hidden');
        window.closeLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.add('hidden');

        window.logoutAdmin = () => {
            if(confirm("관리자 계정에서 로그아웃 하시겠습니까?")) {
                signOut(auth).then(() => location.reload());
            }
        };

        window.loginAdmin = () => {
            const id = document.getElementById('login-id').value;
            const pw = document.getElementById('login-pw').value;
            if (id === 'admin') {
                signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, 'admin@internal.system', pw).then(() => { 
                    closeLoginModal(); 
                }).catch(() => alert('비밀번호가 올바르지 않습니다.'));
            } else { 
                alert('ID 확인 (admin)'); 
            }
        };

        // 실시간 동기화 시작
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('report-list-body');
            
            if (user && user.email === 'admin@internal.system') {
                const reportsRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'reports');
                onSnapshot(reportsRef, (snapshot) => {
                    const reports = [];
                    let pendingCount = 0;
                    let newReportCount = 0;
                    let resolvedCount = 0;

                    snapshot.forEach(doc => {
                        const data = doc.data();
                        reports.push({ id: doc.id, ...data });
                        
                        if (data.status === 'pending') {
                            pendingCount++;
                            if (data.type === 'report') newReportCount++;
                        } else if (data.status === 'resolved') {
                            resolvedCount++;
                        }
                    });

                    // 방어 코드 적용 정렬
                    reports.sort((a, b) => {
                        const tA = (a.createdAt && typeof a.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? a.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                        const tB = (b.createdAt && typeof b.createdAt.toMillis === 'function') ? b.createdAt.toMillis() : 0;
                        return tB - tA;
                    });

                    // 상단 통계 업데이트
                    document.getElementById('stat-total-reports').innerText = reports.length;
                    document.getElementById('stat-pending-reports').innerText = pendingCount;
                    document.getElementById('stat-new-reports').innerText = newReportCount;
                    document.getElementById('stat-resolved-reports').innerText = resolvedCount;
                    document.getElementById('stat-total-bottom').innerText = reports.length;

                    // 사이드바 배지 알림 (미처리 문의/신고 건)
                    const badge = document.getElementById('badge-report-count');
                    if(badge) {
                        if(pendingCount > 0) {
                            badge.innerText = `${pendingCount}건`;
                            badge.classList.remove('hidden');
                        } else {
                            badge.classList.add('hidden');
                        }
                    }

                    // 테이블 리스트 업데이트
                    if (reports.length === 0) {
                        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-6 py-12 text-center text-gray-500 font-medium">아직 접수된 문의나 신고가 없습니다.</td></tr>`;
                    } else {
                        tbody.innerHTML = reports.map(report => {
                            let typeHtml = report.type === 'report' 
                                ? '<span class="px-2 py-1 bg-red-100 text-red-700 rounded text-xs font-bold">신고</span>' 
                                : '<span class="px-2 py-1 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 rounded text-xs font-bold">문의</span>';

                            let statusBadge = report.status === 'pending' 
                                ? '<span class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-red-100 text-red-700 rounded-full text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap">대기 중</span>' 
                                : '<span class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-full text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap">처리 완료</span>';

                            let dateStr = '-';
                            if(report.createdAt && typeof report.createdAt.toDate === 'function') {
                                const d = report.createdAt.toDate();
                                dateStr = `${d.getFullYear()}.${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}.${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(d.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
                            }

                            return `
                                <tr class="border-b border-gray-50 hover:bg-rosegold-50/30 transition ${report.status === 'pending' ? 'bg-red-50/10' : ''}">
                                    <td class="px-6 py-4 text-center"><input type="checkbox" class="rounded border-gray-300 text-rosegold-500 focus:ring-rosegold-500"></td>
                                    <td class="px-6 py-4 text-center">${typeHtml}</td>
                                    <td class="px-6 py-4">
                                        <p class="font-bold text-gray-800 truncate w-48 sm:w-64 md:w-80 cursor-pointer hover:text-rosegold-600 transition" title="${report.content || ''}">${report.title || '제목 없음'}</p>
                                        <p class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 truncate w-48 sm:w-64 md:w-80">${report.content || ''}</p>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="px-6 py-4 text-gray-600 whitespace-nowrap">${report.authorName || '익명'}</td>
                                    <td class="px-6 py-4 text-gray-500 text-xs whitespace-nowrap">${dateStr}</td>
                                    <td class="px-6 py-4">${statusBadge}</td>
                                    <td class="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                                        <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                                            ${report.status === 'pending' ? `
                                                <button onclick="resolveReport('${report.id}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-rosegold-500 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded hover:bg-rosegold-600 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap">답변/처리</button>
                                            ` : `
                                                <button class="text-gray-400 hover:text-rosegold-600 transition" title="내역 보기"><i data-lucide="file-text" class="w-5 h-5 mx-auto"></i></button>
                                            `}
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            `;
                        }).join('');
                        lucide.createIcons();
                    }
                }, (error) => console.error("문의/신고 데이터 가져오기 실패:", error));
            } else {
                if(tbody) tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="px-6 py-20 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium italic">관리자 로그인이 필요합니다. 상단의 로그인 버튼을 눌러주세요.</td></tr>';
                openLoginModal(); // 자동 팝업
            }
        });

        // 전역 함수: 처리 완료 처리
        window.resolveReport = async (reportId) => {
            if(confirm('이 문의/신고를 처리 완료 상태로 변경하시겠습니까?')) {
                try {
                    await updateDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'reports', reportId), {
                        status: 'resolved'
                    });
                    alert('처리 완료되었습니다.');
                } catch(e) {
                    console.error(e);
                    alert('상태 변경에 실패했습니다.');
                }
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

    <!-- [뷰 2] 전용 게시판 목록 화면 -->
    <main id="view-board" class="hidden flex-1 pt-24 sm:pt-28 pb-20 max-w-4xl mx-auto w-full px-4 sm:px-6 animate-[fadeIn_0.3s_ease-out]">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 pb-3 border-b border-gray-200">
            <button onclick="switchToDetail()" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-900 transition">
                <i data-lucide="arrow-left" class="w-4 h-4"></i> 상세 안내로 돌아가기
            </button>
            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border border-emerald-100">정식 멤버 활동 중</span>
        </div>

        <div class="bg-gray-900 text-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 mb-8 shadow-md relative overflow-hidden">
            <h2 id="board-title" class="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black mb-2">동아리 전용 게시판</h2>
            <p class="text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-300">멤버들과 함께 나눈 활동 사진과 소식들을 자유롭게 공유해 보세요.</p>
            <button onclick="openWritePostModal()" class="mt-4 px-4 py-2 bg-white text-gray-900 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-100 transition inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm">
                <i data-lucide="pen-tool" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i> 활동 소식 올리기
            </button>
        </div>

        <div class="space-y-4">
            <h3 class="text-lg font-black text-gray-900 flex items-center justify-between">
                <span>우리들의 활동 기록</span>
                <span class="text-xs text-gray-400 font-normal">카드를 클릭하면 상세한 내용을 확인할 수 있습니다</span>
            </h3>
            <div id="board-posts-container" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 sm:gap-6">
                <div class="col-span-full py-12 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium text-sm">게시글을 불러오는 중입니다...</div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </main>

    <!-- [뷰 3] Trace: 2030 스타일 단독 게시글 상세 열람 화면 -->
    <main id="view-post-detail" class="hidden flex-1 pt-24 sm:pt-28 pb-20 max-w-4xl mx-auto w-full px-4 sm:px-6 animate-[fadeIn_0.3s_ease-out]">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 pb-3 border-b border-gray-200">
            <button onclick="switchToBoard()" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-900 transition">
                <i data-lucide="arrow-left" class="w-4 h-4"></i> 게시판 목록으로 돌아가기
            </button>
            <span class="px-3 py-1 bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">
                활동 기록
            </span>
        </div>

        <h1 id="post-view-title" class="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-gray-900 tracking-tight mb-6 leading-tight break-keep">
            활동 제목
        </h1>

        <!-- 🌟 [핵심 변경] '기록 상태'를 완전히 제거하고 3개 핵심 정보로 정돈한 균형 잡힌 메타 바 -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 bg-white p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-200/80 shadow-sm text-xs mb-8">
            <div>
                <span class="block text-gray-400 font-bold mb-0.5">작성 일자</span>
                <span id="post-view-date" class="font-bold text-gray-900 text-xs sm:text-sm block">-</span>
            </div>
            <div>
                <span class="block text-gray-400 font-bold mb-0.5">소속 동아리</span>
                <span id="post-view-club-name" class="font-bold text-gray-900 text-xs sm:text-sm truncate block">-</span>
            </div>
            <div>
                <span class="block text-gray-400 font-bold mb-0.5">작성자</span>
                <span id="post-view-author" class="font-bold text-gray-900 text-xs sm:text-sm truncate block">-</span>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 갤러리 섹션 -->
        <div id="post-gallery-section" class="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-8 border border-gray-200/80 shadow-sm space-y-4 mb-8">
            <div class="w-full aspect-[16/10] sm:aspect-[16/9] bg-gray-100 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden relative border border-gray-100 shadow-inner">
                <img id="post-view-main-img" src="" alt="현장 사진" class="w-full h-full object-cover transition-opacity duration-300">
            </div>
            <div id="post-view-thumb-row" class="grid grid-cols-4 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-2 sm:gap-3 pt-1"></div>
        </div>

        <!-- 본문 내용 -->
        <div class="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-10 border border-gray-200/80 shadow-sm mb-8 space-y-6">
            <div class="border-l-4 border-rosegold-500 pl-4 py-1">
                <h3 class="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-gray-900">활동 스토리</h3>
            </div>
            <div class="p-6 bg-gray-50/70 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100">
                <p id="post-view-content" class="text-sm sm:text-base text-gray-700 leading-relaxed font-medium whitespace-pre-line break-keep">
                    본문 내용이 표시됩니다.
                </p>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="pt-4 border-t border-gray-200 flex justify-between items-center">
            <button onclick="switchToBoard()" class="px-6 py-3.5 bg-gray-900 text-white rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition">
                ← 목록으로 돌아가기
            </button>
        </div>
    </main>

    <!-- 가입 신청서 모달 -->
    <div id="modal-apply-form" class="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[160] hidden flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-md">
        <div class="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-[2rem] p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl relative">
            <button onclick="closeApplyForm()" class="absolute top-5 right-5 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-900 p-1">
                <i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
            </button>
            <h3 class="text-xl font-black text-gray-900 mb-2">동아리 가입 신청</h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-4">리더에게 전하고 싶은 한마디나 참여 포부를 적어주세요.</p>
            <textarea id="apply-message" rows="4" placeholder="예: 안녕하세요! 이번 주말 모임부터 함께 참여하고 싶습니다." class="w-full p-4 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:border-gray-900 outline-none text-sm resize-none mb-4"></textarea>
            <button type="button" onclick="submitApplication()" class="w-full py-3.5 bg-gray-900 text-white rounded-xl font-black text-sm hover:bg-gray-800 transition">
                신청서 제출하기
            </button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- 활동 소식 작성 모달 -->
    <div id="modal-write-post" class="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[160] hidden flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-md">
        <div class="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-[2rem] p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl relative max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto hide-scrollbar">
            <button onclick="closeWritePostModal()" class="absolute top-5 right-5 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-900 p-1">
                <i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
            </button>
            <h3 class="text-xl font-black text-gray-900 mb-4">활동 소식 남기기</h3>
            <div class="space-y-3.5">
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1">사진 첨부 (대표 1장)</label>
                    <input type="file" id="post-file-input" accept="image/*" class="w-full text-xs text-gray-500 file:mr-2 file:py-2 file:px-3 file:rounded-lg file:border-0 file:bg-gray-100 file:font-bold">
                    <img id="post-file-preview" class="w-full h-32 object-cover rounded-xl mt-2 hidden border border-gray-200">
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1">제목</label>
                    <input type="text" id="post-title-input" placeholder="게시글 제목" class="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold outline-none">
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1">내용</label>
                    <textarea id="post-content-input" rows="5" placeholder="활동 현장 내용과 소감을 자유롭게 적어주세요." class="w-full p-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-sm outline-none resize-none leading-relaxed"></textarea>
                </div>
                <button type="button" onclick="submitPostAction()" class="w-full py-3.5 bg-gray-900 text-white rounded-xl font-black text-sm hover:bg-gray-800 transition mt-2">
                    등록 완료
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <public-footer></public-footer>
    <public-modals></public-modals>

    <script type="module">
        import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-app.js";
        import { getAuth, onAuthStateChanged } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js";
        import { getFirestore, doc, getDoc, collection, onSnapshot, setDoc, addDoc, serverTimestamp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js";

        const firebaseConfig = { 
            apiKey: "AIzaSyAoiX0R0hJoWYqyy52Y6sFWIxwPoljR_7k", 
            authDomain: "talent.unifactory.kr", 
            projectId: "talent-af055", 
            storageBucket: "talent-af055.firebasestorage.app", 
            messagingSenderId: "476130735120", 
            appId: "1:476130735120:web:aae71ccf3e626152e40695", 
            measurementId: "G-1XSNXNNGMH" 
        };

        const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
        const auth = getAuth(app);
        const db = getFirestore(app);
        const appId = 'talent-af055';

        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const clubId = urlParams.get('id');

        let currentClubData = null;
        let galleryImages = [];
        let postCache = {};
        let uploadedPostImageData = null;

        lucide.createIcons();

        if (!clubId) {
            alert("동아리 정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
            location.href = 'club.html';
        }

        // 동아리 상세 정보 가져오기
        const loadClubDetails = async () => {
            try {
                const clubDoc = await getDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs', clubId));
                if (!clubDoc.exists()) {
                    alert("해당 동아리 정보를 찾을 수 없습니다.");
                    location.href = 'club.html';
                    return;
                }

                currentClubData = { id: clubDoc.id, ...clubDoc.data() };
                renderClubDetails(currentClubData);
                checkUserMembership();
            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
            }
        };

        const renderClubDetails = (club) => {
            const title = club.name || club.title || '동아리 모임';
            const catMap = { son: '✂️ 손수 (공예/만들기)', mom: '🏃 몸소 (건강/활동)', etc: '🌻 그 외 나눔' };
            
            document.getElementById('detail-club-title').textContent = title;
            document.getElementById('detail-club-place').textContent = club.place || '기장군 일대';
            document.getElementById('detail-club-leader').textContent = (club.leaderName || club.authorName || '이웃') + ' 님';
            document.getElementById('detail-club-cat-name').textContent = catMap[club.category] || '동아리 모임';
            document.getElementById('detail-category-badge').textContent = catMap[club.category] || '동아리';
            document.getElementById('detail-club-desc').textContent = club.intro || club.description || club.content || '활동 안내 내용이 등록되어 있지 않습니다.';

            galleryImages = [];
            if (club.imageUrl && club.imageUrl.length > 10) galleryImages.push(club.imageUrl);
            if (Array.isArray(club.gallery)) galleryImages.push(...club.gallery);
            if (galleryImages.length === 0) {
                galleryImages = ['https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1517048676732-d65bc937f952?w=800'];
            }

            const mainImg = document.getElementById('detail-main-img');
            mainImg.src = galleryImages[0];

            const thumbRow = document.getElementById('detail-thumb-row');
            thumbRow.innerHTML = galleryImages.map((src, idx) => `
                <button type="button" onclick="selectClubPhoto('${src}')" class="aspect-square rounded-xl overflow-hidden border-2 ${idx === 0 ? 'border-rosegold-500' : 'border-transparent'} hover:opacity-90 transition relative bg-gray-100 shadow-sm thumb-btn">
                    <img src="${src}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">
                </button>
            `).join('');

            lucide.createIcons();
        };

        window.selectClubPhoto = (src) => {
            const mainImg = document.getElementById('detail-main-img');
            mainImg.style.opacity = '0.3';
            setTimeout(() => {
                mainImg.src = src;
                mainImg.style.opacity = '1';
            }, 120);

            document.querySelectorAll('#detail-thumb-row .thumb-btn').forEach(btn => {
                const img = btn.querySelector('img');
                if (img && img.src === src) {
                    btn.classList.add('border-rosegold-500');
                    btn.classList.remove('border-transparent');
                } else {
                    btn.classList.remove('border-rosegold-500');
                    btn.classList.add('border-transparent');
                }
            });
        };

        // 회원 가입 상태 체크
        const checkUserMembership = async () => {
            const user = auth.currentUser;
            const applyBtn = document.getElementById('btn-apply-club');
            const boardBtn = document.getElementById('btn-board-club');

            if (!user || user.isAnonymous) {
                applyBtn.textContent = "로그인 후 가입 신청하기";
                applyBtn.onclick = () => window.openLoginModal();
                boardBtn.classList.add('hidden');
                return;
            }

            if (currentClubData.leaderId === user.uid || user.email === 'admin@internal.system') {
                applyBtn.textContent = "내가 운영 중인 동아리입니다";
                applyBtn.disabled = true;
                applyBtn.classList.add('opacity-70');
                boardBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
                boardBtn.onclick = () => switchToBoard();
                return;
            }

            try {
                const mDoc = await getDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', user.uid, 'memberships', clubId));
                if (mDoc.exists() && mDoc.data().status === 'approved') {
                    applyBtn.textContent = "가입 완료된 정식 멤버입니다";
                    applyBtn.disabled = true;
                    applyBtn.classList.add('opacity-70');
                    boardBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
                    boardBtn.onclick = () => switchToBoard();
                } else if (mDoc.exists() && mDoc.data().status === 'pending') {
                    applyBtn.textContent = "가입 승인 대기 중입니다...";
                    applyBtn.disabled = true;
                    applyBtn.classList.add('opacity-70');
                    boardBtn.classList.add('hidden');
                } else {
                    applyBtn.textContent = "가입 신청하기";
                    applyBtn.disabled = false;
                    applyBtn.classList.remove('opacity-70');
                    applyBtn.onclick = () => openApplyForm();
                    boardBtn.classList.add('hidden');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
            }
        };

        onAuthStateChanged(auth, () => {
            if (currentClubData) checkUserMembership();
        });

        // 뷰 제어 함수군
        window.switchToBoard = () => {
            document.getElementById('view-detail').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('view-post-detail').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('view-board').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('board-title').textContent = (currentClubData.name || currentClubData.title) + " 전용 게시판";
            window.scrollTo(0, 0);
            loadBoardPosts();
        };

        window.switchToDetail = () => {
            document.getElementById('view-board').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('view-post-detail').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('view-detail').classList.remove('hidden');
            window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        };

        // 게시글 상세 열람 (Trace: 2030 스타일: 3열 메타 박스 적용)
        window.openPostDetail = (postId) => {
            const post = postCache[postId];
            if (!post) return;

            document.getElementById('post-view-title').textContent = post.title || '제목 없음';
            document.getElementById('post-view-content').textContent = post.content || '';
            document.getElementById('post-view-author').textContent = (post.authorName || '이웃') + ' 님';
            document.getElementById('post-view-club-name').textContent = currentClubData.name || currentClubData.title || '동아리';

            let dateText = '최근 기록';
            if (post.createdAt && post.createdAt.toDate) {
                const d = post.createdAt.toDate();
                dateText = `${d.getFullYear()}.${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}.${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
            }
            document.getElementById('post-view-date').textContent = dateText;

            // 이미지 갤러리 처리
            const gallerySec = document.getElementById('post-gallery-section');
            const mainImg = document.getElementById('post-view-main-img');
            const thumbRow = document.getElementById('post-view-thumb-row');

            let images = [];
            if (post.imageUrl && post.imageUrl.length > 10) images.push(post.imageUrl);
            if (Array.isArray(post.gallery)) images.push(...post.gallery);

            if (images.length === 0) {
                gallerySec.classList.add('hidden');
            } else {
                gallerySec.classList.remove('hidden');
                mainImg.src = images[0];
                thumbRow.innerHTML = images.map((src, idx) => `
                    <button type="button" onclick="selectPostPhoto('${src}')" class="aspect-square rounded-xl overflow-hidden border-2 ${idx === 0 ? 'border-rosegold-500' : 'border-transparent'} hover:opacity-90 transition relative bg-gray-100 shadow-sm thumb-btn">
                        <img src="${src}" class="w-full h-full object-cover">
                    </button>
                `).join('');
            }

            document.getElementById('view-board').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('view-detail').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('view-post-detail').classList.remove('hidden');
            window.scrollTo(0, 0);
            lucide.createIcons();
        };

        window.selectPostPhoto = (src) => {
            const mainImg = document.getElementById('post-view-main-img');
            mainImg.style.opacity = '0.3';
            setTimeout(() => {
                mainImg.src = src;
                mainImg.style.opacity = '1';
            }, 120);

            document.querySelectorAll('#post-view-thumb-row .thumb-btn').forEach(btn => {
                const img = btn.querySelector('img');
                if (img && img.src === src) {
                    btn.classList.add('border-rosegold-500');
                    btn.classList.remove('border-transparent');
                } else {
                    btn.classList.remove('border-rosegold-500');
                    btn.classList.add('border-transparent');
                }
            });
        };

        // 게시글 실시간 목록 로드
        const loadBoardPosts = () => {
            const postsRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs', clubId, 'posts');
            onSnapshot(postsRef, (snapshot) => {
                const container = document.getElementById('board-posts-container');
                postCache = {};

                if (snapshot.empty) {
                    container.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full py-12 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium text-sm">아직 등록된 활동 소식이 없습니다. 첫 이야기를 남겨보세요! ✨</div>';
                    return;
                }

                container.innerHTML = snapshot.docs.map(docSnap => {
                    const post = { id: docSnap.id, ...docSnap.data() };
                    postCache[docSnap.id] = post;

                    return `
                    <div onclick="openPostDetail('${post.id}')" class="group cursor-pointer bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:border-rosegold-300 hover:-translate-y-1 transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col">
                        ${post.imageUrl ? `<img src="${post.imageUrl}" class="w-full h-44 object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500">` : ''}
                        <div class="p-5 flex flex-col flex-1">
                            <h4 class="font-black text-base text-gray-900 group-hover:text-rosegold-600 transition mb-2 truncate">${post.title || '제목 없음'}</h4>
                            <p class="text-xs text-gray-600 line-clamp-2 mb-4 leading-relaxed font-medium flex-1">${post.content || ''}</p>
                            <div class="pt-3 border-t border-gray-100 mt-auto flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-gray-400">
                                <span>${post.authorName || '이웃'} 님</span>
                                <span class="text-rosegold-500 font-extrabold group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform">자세히 보기 →</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    `;
                }).join('');
                lucide.createIcons();
            });
        };

        // 가입 신청 핸들러
        window.openApplyForm = () => document.getElementById('modal-apply-form').classList.remove('hidden');
        window.closeApplyForm = () => document.getElementById('modal-apply-form').classList.add('hidden');
        window.submitApplication = async () => {
            const user = auth.currentUser;
            const msg = document.getElementById('apply-message').value.trim();
            if (!msg) return alert("신청 포부를 적어주세요!");

            try {
                await setDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', user.uid, 'memberships', clubId), { status: 'pending', joinedAt: serverTimestamp() });
                await addDoc(collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs', clubId, 'applications'), {
                    userId: user.uid,
                    userName: user.displayName || '이웃',
                    message: msg,
                    createdAt: serverTimestamp()
                });
                alert("✅ 가입 신청이 성공적으로 전달되었습니다!\n리더님의 승인을 기다려주세요.");
                closeApplyForm();
                checkUserMembership();
            } catch (err) {
                alert("신청 중 오류가 발생했습니다.");
            }
        };

        // 소식 등록 핸들러 (이미지 리사이징 포함)
        window.openWritePostModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-write-post').classList.remove('hidden');
        window.closeWritePostModal = () => {
            document.getElementById('modal-write-post').classList.add('hidden');
            uploadedPostImageData = null;
            document.getElementById('post-file-preview').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('post-file-input').value = '';
        };

        document.getElementById('post-file-input').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            const file = e.target.files[0];
            if (!file) return;
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (event) => {
                const img = new Image();
                img.onload = () => {
                    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
                    const MAX_WIDTH = 800;
                    let width = img.width;
                    let height = img.height;
                    if (width > MAX_WIDTH) {
                        height = Math.round((height * MAX_WIDTH) / width);
                        width = MAX_WIDTH;
                    }
                    canvas.width = width;
                    canvas.height = height;
                    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
                    uploadedPostImageData = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8);
                    
                    const preview = document.getElementById('post-file-preview');
                    preview.src = uploadedPostImageData;
                    preview.classList.remove('hidden');
                };
                img.src = event.target.result;
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        });

        window.submitPostAction = async () => {
            const user = auth.currentUser;
            const title = document.getElementById('post-title-input').value.trim();
            const content = document.getElementById('post-content-input').value.trim();
            if (!title || !content) return alert("제목과 내용을 모두 적어주세요!");

            try {
                await addDoc(collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs', clubId, 'posts'), {
                    title,
                    content,
                    imageUrl: uploadedPostImageData || '',
                    authorName: user.displayName || '이웃',
                    authorId: user.uid,
                    createdAt: serverTimestamp()
                });
                alert("✅ 소식이 등록되었습니다!");
                closeWritePostModal();
                document.getElementById('post-title-input').value = '';
                document.getElementById('post-content-input').value = '';
            } catch (err) {
                alert("게시글 등록에 실패했습니다.");
            }
        };

        loadClubDetails();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

    <div id="modal-club-reg" class="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[140] hidden flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-md transition-all">
        <div class="bg-white w-full max-w-xl rounded-[2rem] p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl relative max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto hide-scrollbar">

                <i data-lucide="x" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
            </button>
            <h3 class="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-gray-900 mb-5 mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                <i data-lucide="sparkles" class="text-rosegold-500 w-5 h-5"></i> 새 동아리 개설 신청
            </h3>
            <div class="space-y-4">
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1">대표 사진 첨부</label>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-rosegold-500 font-bold mb-2">※ 권장 규격: 1:1 정방형 또는 4:3 가로형</p>
                    <button type="button" onclick="document.getElementById('club-photo-input').click()" class="w-full h-40 bg-gray-50 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 transition group overflow-hidden relative shadow-inner">
                        <i data-lucide="camera" class="w-8 h-8 mb-1 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform"></i>
                        <span class="font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm">사진 선택하기</span>
                        <img id="club-photo-preview" class="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover hidden">
                    </button>
                    <input type="file" id="club-photo-input" class="hidden" accept="image/*">
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1.5">동아리 분류</label>
                    <select id="club-category" class="w-full p-3.5 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:border-gray-900 outline-none font-bold text-sm">
                        <option value="son">✂️ 손수 (공예, 손재주, 요리 등)</option>
                        <option value="mom">🏃 몸소 (운동, 건강, 나들이 등)</option>
                        <option value="etc">🌻 그 외 (취미, 학습, 나눔 등)</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1.5">동아리 명칭</label>
                    <input type="text" id="club-title-input" placeholder="예: 봄맞이 뜨개질 모임" class="w-full p-3.5 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:border-gray-900 outline-none font-bold text-sm">
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1.5">활동 장소</label>
                    <input type="text" id="club-place" placeholder="예: 정관읍 주민복지관 또는 하삼동 카페" class="w-full p-3.5 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:border-gray-900 outline-none font-medium text-sm">
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1.5">소개글 및 활동 안내</label>
                    <textarea id="club-desc" rows="4" placeholder="어떤 활동을 함께 하는지 소개글을 적어주세요." class="w-full p-3.5 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:border-gray-900 outline-none resize-none text-sm leading-relaxed"></textarea>
                </div>
                <button type="button" onclick="submitClub()" class="w-full bg-gray-900 text-white font-black text-base sm:text-lg py-4 rounded-2xl hover:bg-gray-800 transition shadow-md active:scale-95 mt-2">
                    동아리 개설 신청하기
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
