```html
    <!-- 파이어베이스 스크립트 -->
    <script type="module">
        import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-app.js";
        import { getAuth, onAuthStateChanged } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js";
        import { getFirestore, collection, onSnapshot, addDoc, serverTimestamp, getDoc, doc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js";

        const firebaseConfig = { 
            apiKey: "AIzaSyAoiX0R0hJoWYqyy52Y6sFWIxwPoljR_7k", 
            authDomain: "talent.unifactory.kr", 
            projectId: "talent-af055", 
            storageBucket: "talent-af055.firebasestorage.app", 
            messagingSenderId: "476130735120", 
            appId: "1:476130735120:web:aae71ccf3e626152e40695", 
            measurementId: "G-1XSNXNNGMH" 
        };
        
        const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig); 
        const auth = getAuth(app); 
        const db = getFirestore(app); 
        const appId = 'talent-af055'; 

        let currentUploadedImage = null;

        lucide.createIcons();

        // 인증 상태 감지 및 네비게이션 동기화
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login');
            const prof = document.getElementById('user-profile');

            if (user && !user.isAnonymous) {
                if (btn) btn.classList.add('hidden');
                if (prof) { prof.classList.remove('hidden'); prof.classList.add('flex'); }

                let name = user.displayName;
                try {
                    const profileDoc = await getDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', user.uid, 'profile', 'info'));
                    if (profileDoc.exists()) {
                        name = profileDoc.data().nickname || profileDoc.data().name || name;
                    }
                } catch (e) { console.error(e); }

                name = name || '이웃';
                const nameEl = document.getElementById('user-name');
                const avatarEl = document.getElementById('user-avatar');
                if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = name + " 님";
                if (avatarEl) avatarEl.textContent = name.charAt(0);
            } else {
                if (btn) btn.classList.remove('hidden');
                if (prof) prof.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        });

        // 모달 제어
        window.openClubModal = () => {
            if (!auth.currentUser || auth.currentUser.isAnonymous) {
                alert("동아리를 등록하시려면 먼저 로그인해 주세요! 😊");
                if (window.openLoginModal) window.openLoginModal();
            } else {
                document.getElementById('modal-club-reg').classList.remove('hidden');
            }
        };

        window.closeClubModal = () => {
            document.getElementById('modal-club-reg').classList.add('hidden');
            currentUploadedImage = null;
            const preview = document.getElementById('club-photo-preview');
            if (preview) { preview.src = ''; preview.classList.add('hidden'); }
            document.getElementById('club-photo-input').value = '';
        };

        // 이미지 리사이징 업로드 처리
        const handleImageUpload = (file, callback) => {
            if (!file) return;
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (event) => {
                const img = new Image();
                img.onload = () => {
                    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
                    const MAX_WIDTH = 800;
                    let width = img.width;
                    let height = img.height;
                    if (width > MAX_WIDTH) {
                        height = Math.round((height * MAX_WIDTH) / width);
                        width = MAX_WIDTH;
                    }
                    canvas.width = width;
                    canvas.height = height;
                    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
                    callback(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.82));
                };
                img.src = event.target.result;
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        };

        document.getElementById('club-photo-input').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            handleImageUpload(e.target.files[0], (data) => {
                currentUploadedImage = data;
                const preview = document.getElementById('club-photo-preview');
                preview.src = data;
                preview.classList.remove('hidden');
            });
        });

        // 동아리 개설 신청 제출
        window.submitClub = async () => {
            const user = auth.currentUser;
            if (!user || user.isAnonymous) return alert("로그인이 필요합니다.");

            const title = document.getElementById('club-title-input').value.trim();
            const category = document.getElementById('club-category').value;
            const place = document.getElementById('club-place').value.trim();
            const desc = document.getElementById('club-desc').value.trim();

            if (!title || !desc) return alert("동아리 명칭과 소개글을 모두 작성해 주세요!");

            try {
                await addDoc(collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs'), {
                    name: title,
                    title: title,
                    category,
                    place: place || '기장군 일대',
                    intro: desc,
                    description: desc,
                    imageUrl: currentUploadedImage || '',
                    leaderName: user.displayName || '이웃',
                    leaderId: user.uid,
                    createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
                    status: 'active'
                });

                closeClubModal();
                alert("🎉 새 동아리가 성공적으로 등록되었습니다!");
                document.getElementById('club-title-input').value = '';
                document.getElementById('club-place').value = '';
                document.getElementById('club-desc').value = '';
            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
                alert("등록 중 오류가 발생했습니다.");
            }
        };

        // 🌟 [핵심] 카드 클릭 시 activity.html?id=... 로 이동
        const clubsRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs');
        onSnapshot(clubsRef, (snapshot) => {
            const groups = { son: [], mom: [], etc: [] };

            snapshot.forEach(docSnap => {
                const data = docSnap.data();
                if (data.status === 'active') {
                    const item = { id: docSnap.id, ...data };
                    if (groups[data.category]) {
                        groups[data.category].push(item);
                    } else {
                        groups.etc.push(item);
                    }
                }
            });

            ['son', 'mom', 'etc'].forEach(cat => {
                const container = document.getElementById(`club-list-${cat}`);
                if (!container) return;

                if (groups[cat].length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full py-12 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium text-sm">개설된 동아리가 없습니다. 첫 번째 모임을 만들어보세요!</div>';
                } else {
                    container.innerHTML = groups[cat].map(club => {
                        const fallback = 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1517048676732-d65bc937f952?w=800';
                        const imgSrc = (club.imageUrl && club.imageUrl.length > 10) ? club.imageUrl : fallback;
                        const displayTitle = club.name || club.title || '동아리 모임';
                        const displayDesc = club.intro || club.description || club.place || '함께 모여 활동해요!';

                        return `
                        <!-- 🌟 [개선] 레퍼런스 스타일 activity.html 전용 페이지로 이동 -->
                        <div onclick="location.href='activity.html?id=${club.id}'" class="group cursor-pointer bg-white rounded-3xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-100 hover:border-rosegold-300 hover:shadow-xl hover:-translate-y-1.5 transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col h-full">
                            <div class="w-full aspect-[4/3] bg-gray-100 overflow-hidden relative flex-shrink-0">
                                <img src="${imgSrc}" onerror="this.onerror=null; this.src='${fallback}';" class="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500">
                                <div class="absolute top-3 right-3 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-800 shadow-sm">
                                    모집중
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="p-5 flex flex-col flex-1">
                                <h4 class="font-black text-base sm:text-lg text-gray-900 group-hover:text-rosegold-600 transition truncate mb-1">
                                    ${displayTitle}
                                </h4>
                                <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 font-medium line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed mb-4 flex-1">
                                    ${displayDesc}
                                </p>
                                <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-gray-100 mt-auto text-xs font-bold text-gray-400">
                                    <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-gray-600">
                                        <div class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-rosegold-50 text-rosegold-600 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-black">${(club.leaderName || '이').charAt(0)}</div>
                                        <span>${club.leaderName || '이웃'} 님</span>
                                    </div>
                                    <span class="text-rosegold-500 font-extrabold group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform">
                                        활동 보기 →
                                    </span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        `;
                    }).join('');
                }
            });

            lucide.createIcons();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

```javascript
// public/components.js - 부산이음 사용자 공통 웹 컴포넌트

// 1. 공통 네비게이션 헤더
class PublicNav extends HTMLElement {
    connectedCallback() {
        const active = this.getAttribute('active') || 'home';
        
        const getNavLinkClass = (page) => {
            return active === page
                ? "text-gray-900 font-extrabold border-b-2 border-gray-900 pb-1 text-base md:text-lg transition"
                : "text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900 font-medium text-base md:text-lg transition";
        };

        this.innerHTML = `
            <header class="fixed top-0 left-0 right-0 h-20 sm:h-24 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-gray-100 z-50 transition-all shadow-sm">
                <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-full flex items-center justify-between">
                    <!-- 공식 로고 (시원한 크기 적용) -->
                    <a href="index.html" class="flex items-center group py-1 overflow-visible focus:outline-none">
                        <img src="logo.png" alt="부산이음 로고" 
                             class="w-auto object-contain transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:scale-105"
                             style="height: 56px; max-height: 72px; min-height: 48px;">
                    </a>

                    <!-- 메뉴 링크 -->
                    <nav class="hidden md:flex items-center gap-10">
                        <a href="intro.html" class="${getNavLinkClass('intro')}">소개</a>
                        <a href="club.html" class="${getNavLinkClass('club')}">동아리</a>
                        <a href="market.html" class="${getNavLinkClass('market')}">우리기장</a>
                    </nav>

                    <!-- 우측 계정 및 액션 버튼 -->
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4">
                        <button id="btn-login" onclick="window.openLoginModal()" class="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-gray-700 hover:text-gray-900 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl transition">
                            로그인
                        </button>
                        
                        <div id="user-profile" class="hidden items-center gap-2.5 sm:gap-3">
                            <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-rosegold-100 text-rosegold-600 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-black shadow-inner" id="user-avatar">이</div>
                            <span class="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-800" id="user-name">이웃 님</span>
                            <button onclick="window.logout()" class="text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 ml-1 font-medium transition">로그아웃</button>
                        </div>

                        ${active === 'club' ? `
                            <button onclick="window.openClubModal()" class="px-4 sm:px-5 py-2 sm:py-2.5 bg-gray-900 text-white rounded-full text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap active:scale-95">
                                동아리 등록
                            </button>
                        ` : ''}

                        ${active === 'market' ? `
                            <button onclick="window.openRegisterModal()" class="px-4 sm:px-5 py-2 sm:py-2.5 bg-gray-900 text-white rounded-full text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition shadow-sm whitespace-nowrap active:scale-95">
                                소식 올리기
                            </button>
                        ` : ''}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </header>
        `;
        if (window.lucide) window.lucide.createIcons();
    }
}

// 2. 공통 푸터

customElements.define('public-nav', PublicNav);
customElements.define('public-footer', PublicFooter);
customElements.define('public-modals', PublicModals);

// 🌟 [전역 함수 보장] 로그인 & 회원가입 모달 제어 함수
window.openLoginModal = () => {
    const modal = document.getElementById('modal-login');
    if (modal) modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    if (window.lucide) window.lucide.createIcons();
};

window.closeLoginModal = () => {
    const modal = document.getElementById('modal-login');
    if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
};

window.openEmailRegisterModal = () => {
    window.closeLoginModal();
    const modal = document.getElementById('modal-email-register');
    if (modal) modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    if (window.lucide) window.lucide.createIcons();
};

window.closeEmailRegisterModal = () => {
    const modal = document.getElementById('modal-email-register');
    if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
};

window.closeGoogleRegisterModal = () => {
    const modal = document.getElementById('modal-google-register');
    if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
};

window.openResetPasswordModal = () => {
    window.closeLoginModal();
    const modal = document.getElementById('modal-reset-password');
    if (modal) {
        modal.classList.remove('hidden');
        const loginEmail = document.getElementById('login-email');
        const resetEmail = document.getElementById('reset-email');
        if (loginEmail && resetEmail && loginEmail.value) {
            resetEmail.value = loginEmail.value.trim();
        }
    }
    if (window.lucide) window.lucide.createIcons();
};

window.closeResetPasswordModal = () => {
    const modal = document.getElementById('modal-reset-password');
    if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
    const resetEmail = document.getElementById('reset-email');
    if (resetEmail) resetEmail.value = '';
};

// 🌟 [통합 인증 액션] 어떤 페이지에서든 동작하도록 모듈 동적 로드
const getFirebaseAuthInstances = async () => {
    const { initializeApp, getApps } = await import("https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-app.js");
    const { getAuth, GoogleAuthProvider, signInWithPopup, signInWithEmailAndPassword, createUserWithEmailAndPassword, updateProfile, sendPasswordResetEmail, signOut } = await import("https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js");
    const { getFirestore, doc, setDoc, serverTimestamp } = await import("https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js");

    const firebaseConfig = { 
        apiKey: "AIzaSyAoiX0R0hJoWYqyy52Y6sFWIxwPoljR_7k", 
        authDomain: "talent.unifactory.kr", 
        projectId: "talent-af055", 
        storageBucket: "talent-af055.firebasestorage.app", 
        messagingSenderId: "476130735120", 
        appId: "1:476130735120:web:aae71ccf3e626152e40695", 
        measurementId: "G-1XSNXNNGMH" 
    };

    const app = getApps().length > 0 ? getApps()[0] : initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
    const auth = getAuth(app);
    const db = getFirestore(app);
    return { auth, db, GoogleAuthProvider, signInWithPopup, signInWithEmailAndPassword, createUserWithEmailAndPassword, updateProfile, sendPasswordResetEmail, signOut, setDoc, doc, serverTimestamp };
};

// 이메일 로그인
window.loginWithEmail = async () => {
    const emailInput = document.getElementById('login-email');
    const pwInput = document.getElementById('login-pw');
    const email = emailInput ? emailInput.value.trim() : '';
    const password = pwInput ? pwInput.value.trim() : '';

    if (!email || !password) {
        alert("아이디(이메일)와 비밀번호를 모두 입력해 주세요! ✍️");
        return;
    }

    try {
        const { auth, signInWithEmailAndPassword } = await getFirebaseAuthInstances();
        const finalEmail = (email === 'admin') ? 'admin@internal.system' : email;
        await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, finalEmail, password);
        window.closeLoginModal();
        alert("로그인되었습니다! 환영합니다. 🎉");
    } catch (err) {
        console.error("로그인 실패:", err);
        alert("아이디 또는 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다. 다시 확인해 주세요.");
    }
};

// 구글 로그인
window.loginWithGoogle = async () => {
    try {
        const { auth, GoogleAuthProvider, signInWithPopup } = await getFirebaseAuthInstances();
        const provider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
        await signInWithPopup(auth, provider);
        window.closeLoginModal();
    } catch (err) {
        console.error("구글 로그인 실패:", err);
        alert("구글 로그인에 실패했습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.");
    }
};

// 비밀번호 재설정 발송
window.handleSendPasswordReset = async () => {
    const resetInput = document.getElementById('reset-email');
    const email = resetInput ? resetInput.value.trim() : '';

    if (!email) {
        alert("이메일 주소를 입력해 주세요! ✍️");
        if (resetInput) resetInput.focus();
        return;
    }

    try {
        const { auth, sendPasswordResetEmail } = await getFirebaseAuthInstances();
        auth.languageCode = 'ko';
        await sendPasswordResetEmail(auth, email);
        window.closeResetPasswordModal();
        alert(`✅ [${email}]\n해당 이메일로 비밀번호 재설정 링크를 보냈습니다!\n\n※ 메일이 안 보일 경우, 메일함 내의 '스팸 메일함'을 꼭 확인해 주세요.`);
    } catch (err) {
        console.error("비밀번호 재설정 오류:", err);
        let errorMsg = "메일 발송에 실패했습니다. 이메일 주소를 다시 확인해 주세요.";
        if (err.code === 'auth/user-not-found') errorMsg = "등록되어 있지 않은 이메일 주소입니다.";
        else if (err.code === 'auth/invalid-email') errorMsg = "올바른 이메일 주소 형식이 아닙니다.";
        alert(errorMsg);
    }
};

// 로그아웃
window.logout = async () => {
    try {
        const { auth, signOut } = await getFirebaseAuthInstances();
        await signOut(auth);
        alert("안전하게 로그아웃되었습니다.");
        location.reload();
    } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
    }
};
```

```javascript
const appId = typeof __app_id !== 'undefined' ? __app_id : 'talent-af055';

// 🌟 핵심: 다른 HTML 파일들에서 이 변수들을 가져다 쓸 수 있도록 '수출(export)' 합니다.
export { app, db, auth, appId };
```

```html
    <script type="module">
        import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-app.js";
        import { getFirestore, doc, collection, onSnapshot, getDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js";
        import { getAuth, signInAnonymously, onAuthStateChanged } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js";

        const firebaseConfig = { 
            apiKey: "AIzaSyAoiX0R0hJoWYqyy52Y6sFWIxwPoljR_7k", 
            authDomain: "talent.unifactory.kr", 
            projectId: "talent-af055", 
            storageBucket: "talent-af055.firebasestorage.app", 
            messagingSenderId: "476130735120", 
            appId: "1:476130735120:web:aae71ccf3e626152e40695", 
            measurementId: "G-1XSNXNNGMH" 
        };
        
        const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig); 
        const db = getFirestore(app); 
        const auth = getAuth(app); 
        const appId = 'talent-af055'; 
        
        lucide.createIcons();

        // 검색 처리
        window.handleMainSearch = () => {
            const cat = document.getElementById('search-category').value;
            const kw = document.getElementById('search-keyword').value.trim();
            const targetPage = cat === 'market' ? 'market.html' : 'club.html';
            location.href = `${targetPage}?keyword=${encodeURIComponent(kw)}`;
        };

        // 인증 상태 감지
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login');
            const prof = document.getElementById('user-profile');

            if (user && !user.isAnonymous) {
                if(btn) btn.classList.add('hidden'); 
                if(prof) { prof.classList.remove('hidden'); prof.classList.add('flex'); }
                
                let name = user.displayName;
                try {
                    const profileDoc = await getDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', user.uid, 'profile', 'info'));
                    if (profileDoc.exists()) {
                        name = profileDoc.data().nickname || profileDoc.data().name || name;
                    }
                } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
                
                name = name || '이웃';
                window.currentUserName = name;
                const nameEl = document.getElementById('user-name');
                const avatarEl = document.getElementById('user-avatar');
                if(nameEl) nameEl.textContent = name + " 님";
                if(avatarEl) avatarEl.textContent = name.charAt(0);
            } else {
                if(btn) btn.classList.remove('hidden'); 
                if(prof) prof.classList.add('hidden');
                if(!user) { signInAnonymously(auth).catch(() => {}); }
            }
        });

        // 실시간 동아리 & 소식 미리보기 로드
        const loadPreviewData = () => {
            // 1. 동아리 릴레이
            const clubsRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'clubs');
            onSnapshot(clubsRef, (snapshot) => {
                let clubs = [];
                snapshot.forEach(docSnap => {
                    const data = docSnap.data();
                    if(data.status === 'active') clubs.push({ id: docSnap.id, ...data });
                });
                
                const statEl = document.getElementById('stat-club-count');
                if (statEl) statEl.textContent = clubs.length;

                const clubContainer = document.getElementById('preview-club-list');
                if(clubs.length === 0) {
                    clubContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="w-[calc(100vw-2rem)] max-w-7xl mx-auto py-10 text-center text-gray-400 text-sm font-bold bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 whitespace-normal">개설된 동아리가 없습니다.</div>';
                    clubContainer.classList.remove('animate-marquee');
                } else {
                    const fallback = 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1517048676732-d65bc937f952?w=800';
                    const cardsHtml = clubs.map(club => {
                        const imgSrc = (club.imageUrl && club.imageUrl.length > 10) ? club.imageUrl : fallback;
                        return `
                        <div onclick="location.href='activity.html?id=${club.id}'" class="mr-4 sm:mr-5 w-[260px] sm:w-[290px] flex-shrink-0 bg-white border border-gray-100 rounded-3xl p-4 sm:p-5 hover:shadow-xl hover:-translate-y-1.5 transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer group flex flex-col">
                            <div class="w-full h-32 sm:h-40 bg-gray-50 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden relative mb-3.5">
                                <img src="${imgSrc}" onerror="this.onerror=null; this.src='${fallback}';" class="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500">
                                <div class="absolute top-2.5 right-2.5 px-2.5 py-1 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-full text-[10px] font-black text-gray-800 shadow-sm">
                                    모집중
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex flex-col flex-1">
                                <h4 class="font-black text-base sm:text-lg text-gray-900 group-hover:text-rosegold-600 transition truncate mb-2">${club.name || club.title || '동아리'}</h4>
                                <div class="flex items-center justify-between mt-auto pt-3 border-t border-gray-100 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">
                                    <span class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                        <div class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-rosegold-50 text-rosegold-600 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-black">${(club.leaderName || '이').charAt(0)}</div>
                                        ${club.leaderName || '이웃'} 님
                                    </span>
                                    <span class="text-rosegold-500 font-extrabold">활동보기 →</span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        `;
                    }).join('');

                    if (clubs.length >= 3) {
                        clubContainer.innerHTML = cardsHtml + cardsHtml;
                        clubContainer.classList.add('animate-marquee');
                    } else {
                        clubContainer.innerHTML = cardsHtml;
                        clubContainer.classList.remove('animate-marquee');
                    }
                    lucide.createIcons();
                }
            });

            // 2. 최신 우리기장 소식
            const marketRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market');
            onSnapshot(marketRef, (snapshot) => {
                let posts = [];
                snapshot.forEach(docSnap => {
                    const data = docSnap.data();
                    if(data.status !== 'hidden' && data.status !== 'pending') {
                        posts.push({ id: docSnap.id, ...data });
                    }
                });

                const statMarketEl = document.getElementById('stat-market-count');
                if (statMarketEl) statMarketEl.textContent = posts.length;

                posts.sort((a, b) => (b.createdAt?.toMillis ? b.createdAt.toMillis() : 0) - (a.createdAt?.toMillis ? a.createdAt.toMillis() : 0));
                const recentPosts = posts.slice(0, 3);
                const marketContainer = document.getElementById('preview-market-list');

                if(recentPosts.length === 0) {
                    marketContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full py-10 text-center text-gray-400 text-sm font-bold bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200">등록된 동네 소식이 없습니다.</div>';
                } else {
                    const fallback = 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1501339847302-ac426a4a7cbb?w=800';
                    marketContainer.innerHTML = recentPosts.map(post => {
                        const imgSrc = (post.imageUrl && post.imageUrl.length > 10) ? post.imageUrl : fallback;
                        const catMap = { cafe: '☕ 카페 추천', food: '🍽️ 맛집 추천', tour: '🌳 나들이' };
                        return `
                        <div onclick="location.href='market.html'" class="bg-white border border-gray-100 rounded-3xl p-5 hover:shadow-xl hover:-translate-y-1.5 transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer group flex flex-col h-full">
                            <div class="w-full h-36 bg-gray-50 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden relative mb-3.5">
                                <img src="${imgSrc}" onerror="this.onerror=null; this.src='${fallback}';" class="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500">
                                <span class="absolute top-2.5 left-2.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-800 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full shadow-sm">
                                    ${catMap[post.category] || '소식'}
                                </span>
                            </div>
                            <h4 class="font-black text-base sm:text-lg text-gray-900 mb-1.5 truncate group-hover:text-rosegold-600 transition">${post.title}</h4>
                            <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 line-clamp-2 mb-3 flex-1 leading-relaxed font-medium">${post.content}</p>
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-gray-100 mt-auto text-xs font-bold text-gray-400">
                                <span>${post.authorName || '이웃'} 님</span>
                                <span class="text-rosegold-500 font-extrabold">자세히 보기 →</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        `;
                    }).join('');
                    lucide.createIcons();
                }
            });
        };

        // 팝업 배너
        let activePopups = [];
        let currentPopupIndex = 0;
        let popupSlideInterval;

        const startPopupAutoSlide = () => {
            if (popupSlideInterval) clearInterval(popupSlideInterval);
            if (activePopups.length > 1) {
                popupSlideInterval = setInterval(() => { window.nextPopup(); }, 4000);
            }
        };

        const stopPopupAutoSlide = () => {
            if (popupSlideInterval) { clearInterval(popupSlideInterval); popupSlideInterval = null; }
        };

        const loadMainPopup = () => {
            const hideUntil = localStorage.getItem('hideMainPopupUntil');
            if (hideUntil && new Date().getTime() < parseInt(hideUntil)) return;

            const bannersRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'banners');
            onSnapshot(bannersRef, (snapshot) => {
                activePopups = [];
                snapshot.forEach(docSnap => {
                    const data = docSnap.data();
                    if (data.status === 'active') activePopups.push({ id: docSnap.id, ...data });
                });

                activePopups.sort((a, b) => (parseInt(a.order) || 1) - (parseInt(b.order) || 1));

                if (activePopups.length > 0) {
                    currentPopupIndex = 0;
                    renderCurrentPopup();
                    document.getElementById('modal-main-popup').classList.remove('hidden');
                    startPopupAutoSlide();
                }
            });
        };

        window.renderCurrentPopup = () => {
            if (activePopups.length === 0) return;
            const banner = activePopups[currentPopupIndex];
            const imgEl = document.getElementById('popup-image');
            const vidEl = document.getElementById('popup-video');
            const linkEl = document.getElementById('popup-link');
            const fallbackEl = document.getElementById('popup-fallback');

            if (banner.imageUrl) {
                if (banner.mediaType === 'video' || banner.imageUrl.startsWith('data:video')) {
                    imgEl.classList.add('hidden'); vidEl.src = banner.imageUrl; vidEl.classList.remove('hidden');
                } else {
                    vidEl.classList.add('hidden'); imgEl.src = banner.imageUrl; imgEl.classList.remove('hidden');
                }
                fallbackEl.classList.add('hidden');
            } else {
                imgEl.classList.add('hidden'); vidEl.classList.add('hidden'); fallbackEl.classList.remove('hidden');
                document.getElementById('popup-title').innerText = banner.title || '부산이음 공지사항';
            }

            linkEl.href = banner.linkUrl || 'javascript:void(0);';

            const prevBtn = document.getElementById('popup-prev-btn');
            const nextBtn = document.getElementById('popup-next-btn');
            const indicator = document.getElementById('popup-indicator');

            if (activePopups.length > 1) {
                prevBtn.classList.remove('hidden'); nextBtn.classList.remove('hidden'); indicator.classList.remove('hidden');
                indicator.innerText = `${currentPopupIndex + 1} / ${activePopups.length}`;
            } else {
                prevBtn.classList.add('hidden'); nextBtn.classList.add('hidden'); indicator.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        };

        window.prevPopup = () => {
            currentPopupIndex = (currentPopupIndex - 1 + activePopups.length) % activePopups.length;
            renderCurrentPopup(); startPopupAutoSlide();
        };
        window.nextPopup = () => {
            currentPopupIndex = (currentPopupIndex + 1) % activePopups.length;
            renderCurrentPopup(); startPopupAutoSlide();
        };
        window.closeMainPopup = () => {
            if (document.getElementById('popup-hide-today').checked) {
                const tomorrow = new Date().getTime() + (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
                localStorage.setItem('hideMainPopupUntil', tomorrow.toString());
            }
            stopPopupAutoSlide();
            document.getElementById('modal-main-popup').classList.add('hidden');
        };

        loadPreviewData();
        loadMainPopup();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

```html
        window.closeLoginModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-login').classList.add('hidden');
        window.openEmailRegisterModal = () => { closeLoginModal(); document.getElementById('modal-email-register').classList.remove('hidden'); };
        window.closeEmailRegisterModal = () => document.getElementById('modal-email-register').classList.add('hidden');
        
        window.closeGoogleRegisterModal = () => {
            document.getElementById('modal-google-register').classList.add('hidden');
            signOut(auth).then(() => {
                showToast("가입을 취소하여 안전하게 로그아웃 되었습니다.");
            });
        };

        // 🌟 사용자 인증 상태 감지
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login');
            const prof = document.getElementById('user-profile');
            const mobileLogoutBtn = document.getElementById('mobile-logout-btn');

            if (user && !user.isAnonymous) {
                if(btn) btn.classList.add('hidden'); 
                if(prof) { prof.classList.remove('hidden'); prof.classList.add('flex'); }
                if(mobileLogoutBtn) mobileLogoutBtn.classList.remove('hidden'); 
                
                let name = user.displayName;
                let isProfileComplete = false;
                
                try {
                    const profileDoc = await getDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', user.uid, 'profile', 'info'));
                    if (profileDoc.exists()) {
                        isProfileComplete = true;
                        name = profileDoc.data().nickname || profileDoc.data().name || name;
                    }
                } catch (e) { console.error("프로필 정보 조회 실패:", e); }
                
                if (!isProfileComplete && user.email !== 'admin@internal.system') {
                    window.tempGoogleUser = user;
                    const nameInput = document.getElementById('g-reg-name');
                    if(nameInput) nameInput.value = user.displayName || '';
                    document.getElementById('modal-google-register').classList.remove('hidden');
                } else if (isProfileComplete) {
                    document.getElementById('modal-google-register').classList.add('hidden');
                }
                
                if (!name) {
                    if (user.email === 'admin@internal.system') name = '최고관리자';
                    else if (user.email) name = user.email.split('@')[0];
                    else name = '이웃';
                }
                
                window.currentUserName = name;
                const nameEl = document.getElementById('user-name');
                const avatarEl = document.getElementById('user-avatar');
                
                if(nameEl) nameEl.textContent = name + " 님";
                if(avatarEl) avatarEl.textContent = name.charAt(0);
            } else {
                if(btn) btn.classList.remove('hidden'); 
                if(prof) prof.classList.add('hidden');
                if(mobileLogoutBtn) mobileLogoutBtn.classList.add('hidden'); 
                if(!user) signInAnonymously(auth).catch(console.error);
            }
        });

        // 🌟 로그인/가입 기능 로직
        window.loginWithGoogle = async () => {
            try {
                await signInWithPopup(auth, provider);
                closeLoginModal();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
                showToast("구글 로그인에 실패했습니다.");
            }
        };

        window.submitGoogleRegister = async () => {
            const nameVal = document.getElementById('g-reg-name').value;
            const nicknameVal = document.getElementById('g-reg-nickname').value;
            const phoneVal = document.getElementById('g-reg-phone').value;
            
            if(!nameVal || !nicknameVal || !phoneVal) return showToast("정보를 모두 입력해주세요!");
            
            try {
                const user = window.tempGoogleUser || auth.currentUser;
                await updateProfile(user, { displayName: nicknameVal });
                await setDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', user.uid, 'profile', 'info'), { 
                    email: user.email || '', name: nameVal, nickname: nicknameVal, phone: phoneVal, createdAt: serverTimestamp(), status: 'active' 
                });
                document.getElementById('modal-google-register').classList.add('hidden');
                showToast("가입 축하합니다! 🥳");
                
                window.currentUserName = nicknameVal;
                const nameEl = document.getElementById('user-name');
                const avatarEl = document.getElementById('user-avatar');
                if(nameEl) nameEl.textContent = nicknameVal + " 님";
                if(avatarEl) avatarEl.textContent = nicknameVal.charAt(0);
            } catch(err) { showToast("저장 실패: " + err.message); }
        };
        
        window.loginWithEmail = () => {
            const idInput = document.getElementById('login-email').value;
            const p = document.getElementById('login-pw').value;
            if(!idInput || !p) return showToast("아이디와 비밀번호를 모두 입력해 주세요!");

            signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, (idInput === 'admin') ? 'admin@internal.system' : idInput, p)
                .then(() => { closeLoginModal(); showToast(idInput === 'admin' ? "최고관리자로 로그인되었습니다. ✨" : "로그인 성공! 🎉"); })
                .catch(() => showToast("아이디나 비밀번호를 다시 확인해주세요."));
        };

        window.submitEmailRegister = async () => {
            const e = document.getElementById('reg-email').value, p = document.getElementById('reg-pw').value, n = document.getElementById('reg-name').value, nk = document.getElementById('reg-nickname').value, ph = document.getElementById('reg-phone').value;
            
            if(!e || !p || !n || !nk || !ph) return showToast("모든 정보를 입력하셔야 가입이 가능합니다!");
            
            try {
                const uc = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth, e, p);
                await updateProfile(uc.user, { displayName: nk });
                await setDoc(doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', uc.user.uid, 'profile', 'info'), { 
                    email: e, name: n, nickname: nk, phone: ph, createdAt: serverTimestamp(), status: 'active' 
                });
                closeEmailRegisterModal(); 
                showToast("가입 축하합니다! 🥳");
            } catch(err) { showToast("가입 실패: 이미 있는 이메일이거나 형식이 잘못되었습니다."); }
        };

        window.logout = () => {
            signOut(auth).then(() => { showToast("로그아웃 되었습니다."); location.reload(); });
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

```html
        const db = getFirestore(app);

        window.showToast = (msg) => {

            t.textContent = msg; 
            t.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none'); 
            t.classList.add('opacity-100'); 
            setTimeout(() => { 
                t.classList.remove('opacity-100'); 
                t.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none'); 
            }, 3000);

        window.openRegisterModal = () => {

                if (window.openLoginModal) window.openLoginModal(); 
            } else { 
                document.getElementById('modal-register').classList.remove('hidden'); 
            }
        };

        window.closeRegisterModal = () => { 
            document.getElementById('modal-register').classList.add('hidden'); 
            currentPostImageData = null;
            const preview = document.getElementById('post-photo-preview');
            if (preview) { preview.src = ''; preview.classList.add('hidden'); }
            document.getElementById('post-photo-input').value = '';

            document.getElementById('detail-title').textContent = item.title || '제목 없음';
            document.getElementById('detail-content').textContent = item.content || '내용이 없습니다.';
            document.getElementById('detail-author').textContent = (item.authorName || '이웃') + ' 님';
            
            // 날짜 포맷팅
            let dateText = '최근 소식';
            if (item.createdAt && item.createdAt.toDate) {
                const d = item.createdAt.toDate();
                dateText = `${d.getFullYear()}.${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}.${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
            }
            document.getElementById('detail-date').textContent = dateText;

            // 카테고리 배지 텍스트
            const catMap = { cafe: '☕ 카페 추천', food: '🍽️ 맛집 추천', tour: '🌳 나들이 추천' };
            document.getElementById('detail-category-badge').textContent = catMap[item.category] || '소식';

            // 이미지 세팅
            const fallback = getFallbackImage(item.category);
            const imgSrc = (item.imageUrl && item.imageUrl.trim().length > 10) ? item.imageUrl : fallback;
            const imgEl = document.getElementById('detail-image');
            imgEl.src = imgSrc;
            imgEl.onerror = () => { imgEl.src = fallback; };

            document.getElementById('modal-detail').classList.remove('hidden');
            lucide.createIcons();
        };

        window.closeDetailModal = () => {
            document.getElementById('modal-detail').classList.add('hidden');
        };

        // 사진 자동 최적화 압축 업로드 (가로 800px, 80% 압축)
        const handleImageUpload = (file, callback) => {
            if (!file) return;
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (event) => {
                const img = new Image();
                img.onload = () => {
                    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
                    const MAX_WIDTH = 800;
                    let width = img.width;
                    let height = img.height;
                    if (width > MAX_WIDTH) {
                        height = Math.round((height * MAX_WIDTH) / width);
                        width = MAX_WIDTH;
                    }
                    canvas.width = width;
                    canvas.height = height;
                    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
                    callback(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.82));
                };
                img.src = event.target.result;
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        };

        document.getElementById('post-photo-input').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            handleImageUpload(e.target.files[0], (compressedData) => {
                currentPostImageData = compressedData;
                const preview = document.getElementById('post-photo-preview');
                preview.src = compressedData;
                preview.classList.remove('hidden');
            });
        });

        // 소식 등록 실행
        window.submitPost = async () => {
            const user = auth.currentUser; 
            if(!user || user.isAnonymous) return alert("로그인이 필요합니다.");
            
            const titleVal = document.getElementById('post-title').value.trim();
            const categoryVal = document.getElementById('post-category').value;
            const contentVal = document.getElementById('post-content').value.trim();
            
            if(!titleVal || !contentVal) { return alert("제목과 상세 이야기를 모두 적어주세요! ✍️"); }
            
            try {
                const postPayload = { 
                    title: titleVal, 
                    category: categoryVal, 
                    content: contentVal, 
                    imageUrl: currentPostImageData || '',
                    authorName: user.displayName || "이웃", 
                    authorId: user.uid, 
                    createdAt: serverTimestamp(), 
                    likes: 0, 
                    status: 'pending' // 관리자 승인 대기
                };

                await addDoc(collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market'), postPayload);
                
                closeRegisterModal(); 
                alert("✅ 소식이 성공적으로 전해졌어요!\n관리자 확인 후 사이트에 정식 공개됩니다. 💌");
                document.getElementById('post-title').value = ''; 
                document.getElementById('post-content').value = '';
            } catch(e) { 
                alert("등록 중 오류가 발생했습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요."); 
                console.error(e); 
            }
        };

        // 기본 안전 대체 이미지
        const getFallbackImage = (cat) => {
            if (cat === 'cafe') return 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1501339847302-ac426a4a7cbb?w=800';
            if (cat === 'food') return 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1555396273-367ea4eb4db5?w=800';
            return 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1507525428034-b723cf961d3e?w=800';
        };

        // 실시간 소식 수신 및 렌더링
        const marketRef = collection(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'public', 'data', 'market');
        onSnapshot(marketRef, (snapshot) => {
            const lists = { cafe: [], food: [], tour: [] };
            window.marketItemsCache = {};
            
            snapshot.forEach(docSnap => { 
                const data = docSnap.data(); 
                if (data.status !== 'hidden' && data.status !== 'pending') { 
                    const itemData = { id: docSnap.id, ...data };
                    window.marketItemsCache[docSnap.id] = itemData;
                    if (lists[data.category]) { 
                        lists[data.category].push(itemData); 
                    }
                } 
            });

            Object.keys(lists).forEach(cat => {
                const container = document.getElementById(`market-list-${cat}`);
                if (!container) return;
                
                if (lists[cat].length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = '<div class="col-span-full py-12 text-center text-gray-400 font-medium text-sm">등록된 소식이 없습니다. 첫 번째 이야기를 남겨보세요!</div>';
                } else {
                    container.innerHTML = lists[cat].map(item => {
                        const fallback = getFallbackImage(cat);
                        const validImageUrl = (item.imageUrl && item.imageUrl.trim().length > 10) ? item.imageUrl : fallback;

                        return `
                        <!-- 🌟 카드 클릭 시 openDetailModal('${item.id}') 실행 -->
                        <div onclick="openDetailModal('${item.id}')" class="group cursor-pointer bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-100 hover:border-rosegold-300 hover:shadow-xl hover:-translate-y-1 transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col h-full">
                            <div class="w-full aspect-[4/3] bg-gray-100 overflow-hidden relative flex-shrink-0">
                                <img src="${validImageUrl}" onerror="this.onerror=null; this.src='${fallback}';" class="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500">
                                <div class="absolute top-2.5 right-2.5 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold">
                                    자세히 보기 🔍
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-col flex-1">
                                <h4 class="font-black text-base text-gray-900 group-hover:text-rosegold-600 transition truncate mb-1.5">${item.title}</h4>
                                <!-- 미리보기 요약 (2줄) -->
                                <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-relaxed line-clamp-2 mb-3 font-medium flex-1">${item.content}</p>
                                <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-gray-100 mt-auto text-[11px] text-gray-400 font-bold">
                                    <span>${item.authorName || '이웃'} 님</span>
                                    <span class="text-rosegold-500 font-extrabold">전체 읽기 →</span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `}).join('');
                }
            });
            
            lucide.createIcons();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```
